   52: export default function CurriculumSettingsPage() {
   53:   const router = useRouter();
   54: 
   55:   const { user, profile, loading, profileReady, profileError, refreshProfile } =
   56:     useAuth();
   57: 
   58:   const [qualification, setQualification] = useState<Qualification>("GCSE");
   59: 
   60:   const [examBoard, setExamBoard] = useState<ExamBoard>("AQA");
   61: 
   62:   const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
   63:   const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);
   64:   const [error, setError] = useState("");
   65: 
   66:   useEffect(() => {
   67:     if (!loading && !user) {
   68:       router.replace("/login");
   69:       return;
   70:     }
   71: 
   72:     if (!loading && profileReady && profile && profile.role !== "student") {
   73:       router.replace(profile.role === "admin" ? "/admin" : "/teacher");
   74:       return;
   75:     }
   76: 
   77:     if (
   78:       profile?.qualification === "GCSE" ||
   79:       profile?.qualification === "A_LEVEL"
   80:     ) {
   81:       setQualification(profile.qualification);
   82:     }
   83: 
   84:     if (
   85:       profile?.examBoard === "AQA" ||
   86:       profile?.examBoard === "OCR" ||
   87:       profile?.examBoard === "EDEXCEL"
   88:     ) {
   89:       setExamBoard(profile.examBoard);
   90:     }
   91:   }, [loading, user, profileReady, profile, router]);
   92: 
   93:   async function handleSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
   94:     event.preventDefault();
   95: 
   96:     if (!user) {
   97:       setError("You must be signed in to change your curriculum.");
   98:       return;
   99:     }
  100: 
  101:     if (
  102:       !getCurriculumDefinition(
  103:         qualification,
  104:         examBoard,
  105:       )
  106:     ) {
  107:       setError(
  108:         "This qualification and exam-board combination is not currently published.",
  109:       );
  110:       return;
  111:     }
  112: 
  113:     setSubmitting(true);
  114:     setSaved(false);
  115:     setError("");
  116: 
  117:     try {
  118:       await updateUserCourseSelection(user.uid, {
  119:         qualification,
  120:         examBoard,
  121:       });
  122: 
  123:       const refreshedProfile = await refreshProfile();
  124: 
  125:       if (
  126:         !refreshedProfile ||
  127:         refreshedProfile.qualification !== qualification ||
  128:         refreshedProfile.examBoard !== examBoard ||
  129:         refreshedProfile.onboardingComplete !== true
  130:       ) {
  131:         throw new Error(
  132:           "The curriculum was saved, but the updated profile could not be confirmed.",
  133:         );
  134:       }
  135: 
  136:       setSaved(true);
  137: 
  138:       router.replace("/learn");
  139:     } catch (caughtError) {
  140:       console.error("Unable to update curriculum:", caughtError);
  141: 
  142:       setError(
  143:         caughtError instanceof Error
  144:           ? caughtError.message
  145:           : "Your curriculum could not be updated.",
  146:       );
  147:     } finally {
  148:       setSubmitting(false);
  149:     }
  150:   }
  151: 
  152:   if (loading || (user && !profileReady && !profileError)) {
  153:     return (
  154:       <div className="flex min-h-[60vh] items-center justify-center">
  155:         <div className="text-center">
  156:           <div className="mx-auto h-12 w-12 animate-spin rounded-full border-4 border-blue-100 border-t-blue-600" />
  157: 
  158:           <p className="mt-4 font-bold text-slate-700">
  159:             Loading curriculum settings...
  160:           </p>
  161:         </div>
  162:       </div>
  163:     );
  164:   }
  165: 
  166:   return (
  167:     <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl space-y-8">
  168:       <section className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-8 shadow-sm md:p-10">
  169:         <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-6 md:flex-row md:items-start">
  170:           <div>
  171:             <p className="text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-blue-600">
  172:               Curriculum settings
  173:             </p>
  174: 
  175:             <h1 className="mt-2 text-4xl font-black text-slate-950">
  176:               Change your Computer Science course
  177:             </h1>
  178: 
  179:             <p className="mt-4 max-w-3xl leading-7 text-slate-600">
  180:               Your selection controls the units, topics, lessons, quizzes and
  181:               exam-board content shown throughout CS Master.
  182:             </p>
  183:           </div>
  184: 
  185:           <Link
  186:             href="/learn"
  187:             className="inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-5 py-3 font-black text-slate-700 transition hover:bg-slate-50"
  188:           >
  189:             Back to Learn
  190:           </Link>
  191:         </div>
  192: 
  193:         {error && (
  194:           <div
  195:             role="alert"
  196:             className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4 font-semibold text-red-800"
  197:           >
  198:             {error}
  199:           </div>
  200:         )}
  201: 
  202:         {saved && (
  203:           <div
  204:             role="status"
  205:             className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 p-4 font-semibold text-emerald-800"
  206:           >
  207:             Your curriculum has been updated.
  208:           </div>
  209:         )}
  210: 
  211:         <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="mt-10 space-y-10">
  212:           <fieldset>
  213:             <legend className="text-xl font-black text-slate-950">
  214:               1. Qualification
  215:             </legend>
  216: 
  217:             <div className="mt-5 grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
  218:               {qualificationOptions.map((option) => {
  219:                 const selected = qualification === option.value;
  220: 
  221:                 return (
  222:                   <label
  223:                     key={option.value}
  224:                     className={`cursor-pointer rounded-2xl border-2 p-6 transition ${
  225:                       selected
  226:                         ? "border-blue-600 bg-blue-50"
  227:                         : "border-slate-200 bg-white hover:border-blue-300"
  228:                     }`}
  229:                   >
  230:                     <input
  231:                       type="radio"
  232:                       name="qualification"
  233:                       value={option.value}
  234:                       checked={selected}
  235:                       onChange={() => setQualification(option.value)}
  236:                       className="sr-only"
  237:                     />
  238: 
  239:                     <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
  240:                       <div>
  241:                         <p className="text-xl font-black text-slate-950">
  242:                           {option.title}
  243:                         </p>
  244: 
  245:                         <p className="mt-2 leading-7 text-slate-600">
  246:                           {option.description}
  247:                         </p>
  248:                       </div>
  249: 
  250:                       <span
  251:                         className={`mt-1 h-6 w-6 shrink-0 rounded-full border-2 ${
  252:                           selected
  253:                             ? "border-blue-600 bg-blue-600 ring-4 ring-blue-100"
  254:                             : "border-slate-300"
  255:                         }`}
  256:                       />
  257:                     </div>
  258:                   </label>
  259:                 );
  260:               })}
  261:             </div>
  262:           </fieldset>
  263: 
  264:           <fieldset>
  265:             <legend className="text-xl font-black text-slate-950">
  266:               2. Exam board
  267:             </legend>
  268: 
  269:             <div className="mt-5 grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
  270:               {examBoardOptions.map((option) => {
  271:                 const selected = examBoard === option.value;
  272: 
  273:                 return (
  274:                   <label
  275:                     key={option.value}
  276:                     className={`cursor-pointer rounded-2xl border-2 p-6 transition ${
  277:                       selected
  278:                         ? "border-indigo-600 bg-indigo-50"
  279:                         : "border-slate-200 bg-white hover:border-indigo-300"
  280:                     }`}
  281:                   >
  282:                     <input
  283:                       type="radio"
  284:                       name="examBoard"
  285:                       value={option.value}
  286:                       checked={selected}
  287:                       onChange={() => setExamBoard(option.value)}
  288:                       className="sr-only"
  289:                     />
  290: 
  291:                     <p className="text-xl font-black text-slate-950">
  292:                       {option.title}
  293:                     </p>
  294: 
  295:                     <p className="mt-2 leading-7 text-slate-600">
  296:                       {option.description}
  297:                     </p>
  298:                   </label>
  299:                 );
  300:               })}
  301:             </div>
  302:           </fieldset>
  303: 
  304:           {!getCurriculumDefinition(
  305:             qualification,
  306:             examBoard,
  307:           ) && (
  308:             <div className="rounded-2xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 p-5 font-semibold text-amber-900">
  309:               This qualification and exam-board combination is not currently
  310:               published in CS Master. Choose an available curriculum.
  311:             </div>
  312:           )}
  313: 
  314:           <button
  315:             type="submit"
  316:             disabled={
  317:               submitting ||
  318:               !getCurriculumDefinition(
  319:                 qualification,
  320:                 examBoard,
  321:               )
  322:             }
  323:             className="w-full rounded-2xl bg-blue-600 px-6 py-4 text-lg font-black text-white transition hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-slate-300"
  324:           >
  325:             {submitting ? "Updating curriculum..." : "Save curriculum"}
  326:           </button>
  327:         </form>
  328:       </section>
  329:     </div>
  330:   );
  331: }

    1: "use client";

    5:   useRouter,

    9:   doc,
   10:   getDoc,
   11: } from "firebase/firestore";

   14: import Card from "@/components/ui/Card";

   21: 
   22: import {
   23:   getCurriculumQuizByTopic,
   24:   getCurriculumQuizzes,
   25:   getCurriculumTopicsWithoutQuizzes,
   26: } from "@/data/quizzes/quizRegistry";
   27: 
   28: import { db } from "@/lib/firebase";
   29:

   31: import type {
   32:   ExamBoard,
   33:   Qualification,

   35: 
   36: type SavedQuizQuestion = {
   37:   id?: string;
   38:   type?: string;
   39:   question?: string;
   40:   options?: string[];
   41:   correctAnswer?: string;
   42:   explanation?: string;
   43:   xpReward?: number;
   44: };
   45: 
   46: function normaliseLookupValue(
   47:   value: string,
   48: ): string {
   49:   return value
   50:     .trim()
   51:     .toLowerCase()
   52:     .replace(/&/g, "and")
   53:     .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, " ")
   54:     .trim();
   55: }
   56: 
   57: /*
   58:  * Query strings may contain either:
   59:  *
   60:  *   /quiz?topic=binary
   61:  *
   62:  * or a human-readable adaptive-learning topic:
   63:  *
   64:  *   /quiz?topic=Binary%20Numbers
   65:  *
   66:  * Resolve both forms through the canonical curriculum registry.
   67:  */
   68: function resolveCurriculumTopicId(
   69:   value: string,
   70: ): string | null {
   71:   const cleaned =
   72:     normaliseLookupValue(value);
   73: 
   74:   if (!cleaned) {
   75:     return null;
   76:   }
   77: 
   78:   const match =
   79:     curriculumRegistry.find(
   80:       (topic) => {
   81:         const searchable = [
   82:           topic.id,
   83:           topic.title,
   84:           ...(topic.aliases || []),
   85:         ].map(
   86:           normaliseLookupValue,
   87:         );
   88: 
   89:         return searchable.includes(
   90:           cleaned,
   91:         );
   92:       },
   93:     );
   94: 
   95:   return match?.id || null;
   96: }
   97: 
   98: export default function QuizPage() {
   99:   const router = useRouter();
  100:   const searchParams =
  101:     useSearchParams();
  102: 
  103:   const {
  104:     profile,
  105:     loading: authLoading,
  106:     profileReady,
  107:   } = useAuth();
  108: 
  109:   const topicParam =
  110:     searchParams.get("topic");
  111: 
  112:   const assignmentId =
  113:     searchParams.get(
  114:       "assignment",
  115:     );
  116: 
  117:   const [
  118:     selectedQuizId,
  119:     setSelectedQuizId,
  120:   ] = useState<
  121:     string | null
  122:   >(null);
  123: 
  124:   const [
  125:     generatedQuiz,
  126:     setGeneratedQuiz,
  127:   ] = useState<Quiz | null>(
  128:     null,
  129:   );
  130: 
  131:   const [
  132:     loadingGeneratedQuiz,
  133:     setLoadingGeneratedQuiz,
  134:   ] = useState(false);
  135: 
  136:   const [
  137:     loadError,
  138:     setLoadError,
  139:   ] = useState("");
  140: 
  141:   const qualification =
  142:     profile?.qualification as
  143:       | Qualification
  144:       | undefined;
  145: 
  146:   const examBoard =
  147:     profile?.examBoard as
  148:       | ExamBoard
  149:       | undefined;
  150: 
  151:   /*
  152:    * The visible Quiz Centre is now derived from the
  153:    * student's selected curriculum.
  154:    */
  155:   const curriculumQuizzes =
  156:     useMemo(() => {
  157:       if (
  158:         !qualification ||
  159:         !examBoard
  160:       ) {
  161:         return [];
  162:       }
  163: 
  164:       return getCurriculumQuizzes(
  165:         qualification,
  166:         examBoard,
  167:       );
  168:     }, [
  169:       qualification,
  170:       examBoard,
  171:     ]);
  172: 
  173:   /*
  174:    * Useful while we are still authoring the full quiz bank.
  175:    * This lets the UI communicate that the curriculum may
  176:    * contain topics whose quizzes have not yet been published.
  177:    */
  178:   const unpublishedQuizTopics =
  179:     useMemo(() => {
  180:       if (
  181:         !qualification ||
  182:         !examBoard
  183:       ) {
  184:         return [];
  185:       }
  186: 
  187:       return getCurriculumTopicsWithoutQuizzes(
  188:         qualification,
  189:         examBoard,
  190:       );
  191:     }, [
  192:       qualification,
  193:       examBoard,
  194:     ]);
  195: 
  196:   const builtInQuiz =
  197:   selectedQuizId &&
  198:   qualification &&
  199:   examBoard
  200:     ? getCurriculumQuizByTopic(
  201:         selectedQuizId,
  202:         qualification,
  203:         examBoard,
  204:       )
  205:     : null;
  206: 
  207:   const selectedQuiz =
  208:     generatedQuiz ||
  209:     builtInQuiz;
  210: 
  211:   useEffect(() => {
  212:     if (
  213:       authLoading ||
  214:       !profileReady
  215:     ) {
  216:       return;
  217:     }
  218: 
  219:     if (!topicParam) {
  220:       setGeneratedQuiz(null);
  221:       setSelectedQuizId(
  222:         null,
  223:       );
  224:       setLoadError("");
  225:       return;
  226:     }
  227: 
  228:     /*
  229:      * ----------------------------------------------------------
  230:      * BUILT-IN CURRICULUM QUIZ
  231:      * ----------------------------------------------------------
  232:      *
  233:      * Resolve both canonical topic IDs and human-readable topic
  234:      * names/aliases.
  235:      */
  236:     const resolvedTopicId =
  237:       resolveCurriculumTopicId(
  238:         topicParam,
  239:       );
  240: 
  241:     if (
  242:       resolvedTopicId &&
  243:       qualification &&
  244:       examBoard
  245:     ) {
  246:       const curriculumQuiz =
  247:         getCurriculumQuizByTopic(
  248:           resolvedTopicId,
  249:           qualification,
  250:           examBoard,
  251:         );
  252: 
  253:       if (curriculumQuiz) {
  254:         setSelectedQuizId(
  255:           resolvedTopicId,
  256:         );
  257: 
  258:         setGeneratedQuiz(
  259:           null,
  260:         );
  261: 
  262:         setLoadError("");
  263: 
  264:         return;
  265:       }
  266:     }
  267: 
  268:     /*
  269:      * ----------------------------------------------------------
  270:      * GENERATED / TEACHER-ASSIGNED QUIZ
  271:      * ----------------------------------------------------------
  272:      *
  273:      * A generated quiz does not have to exist in quizLibrary.
  274:      * Its Firestore document ID is supplied through ?topic=.
  275:      *
  276:      * This preserves the existing teacher assignment workflow.
  277:      */
  278:     let cancelled =
  279:       false;
  280: 
  281:     async function loadGeneratedQuiz() {
  282:       setLoadingGeneratedQuiz(
  283:         true,
  284:       );
  285: 
  286:       setLoadError("");
  287: 
  288:       setSelectedQuizId(
  289:         null,
  290:       );
  291: 
  292:       try {
  293:         const quizSnapshot =
  294:           await getDoc(
  295:             doc(
  296:               db,
  297:               "generatedQuizzes",
  298:               topicParam as string,
  299:             ),
  300:           );
  301: 
  302:         if (cancelled) {
  303:           return;
  304:         }
  305: 
  306:         if (
  307:           !quizSnapshot.exists()
  308:         ) {
  309:           setGeneratedQuiz(
  310:             null,
  311:           );
  312: 
  313:           /*
  314:            * If this looked like a genuine curriculum topic but
  315:            * no built-in quiz exists yet, give the learner a
  316:            * meaningful message rather than pretending a generated
  317:            * quiz should exist.
  318:            */
  319:           if (
  320:             resolvedTopicId
  321:           ) {
  322:             setLoadError(
  323:               "A quiz for this curriculum topic has not been published yet.",
  324:             );
  325:           } else {
  326:             setLoadError(
  327:               "The assigned quiz could not be found.",
  328:             );
  329:           }
  330: 
  331:           return;
  332:         }
  333: 
  334:         const data =
  335:           quizSnapshot.data();
  336: 
  337:         const rawQuestions:
  338:           SavedQuizQuestion[] =
  339:           Array.isArray(
  340:             data.questions,
  341:           )
  342:             ? data.questions
  343:             : [];
  344: 
  345:         const questions =
  346:           rawQuestions
  347:             .filter(
  348:               (question) =>
  349:                 typeof question.question ===
  350:                   "string" &&
  351:                 Array.isArray(
  352:                   question.options,
  353:                 ) &&
  354:                 typeof question.correctAnswer ===
  355:                   "string",
  356:             )
  357:             .map(
  358:               (
  359:                 question,
  360:                 index,
  361:               ) => ({
  362:                 id:
  363:                   question.id ||
  364:                   `${quizSnapshot.id}-question-${index + 1}`,
  365: 
  366:                 type:
  367:                   "multipleChoice" as const,
  368: 
  369:                 question:
  370:                   question.question ||
  371:                   `Question ${index + 1}`,
  372: 
  373:                 options:
  374:                   question.options ||
  375:                   [],
  376: 
  377:                 correctAnswer:
  378:                   question.correctAnswer ||
  379:                   "",
  380: 
  381:                 explanation:
  382:                   question.explanation ||
  383:                   "Review this topic with your teacher.",
  384: 
  385:                 xpReward:
  386:                   typeof question.xpReward ===
  387:                   "number"
  388:                     ? question.xpReward
  389:                     : 10,
  390:               }),
  391:             );
  392: 
  393:         if (
  394:           questions.length ===
  395:           0
  396:         ) {
  397:           setGeneratedQuiz(
  398:             null,
  399:           );
  400: 
  401:           setLoadError(
  402:             "This quiz does not contain any valid questions.",
  403:           );
  404: 
  405:           return;
  406:         }
  407: 
  408:         const loadedQuiz:
  409:           Quiz = {
  410:           id:
  411:             quizSnapshot.id,
  412: 
  413:           /*
  414:            * Prefer stored curriculum metadata if the generated
  415:            * quiz has it. Fall back to the document ID for legacy
  416:            * generated quizzes.
  417:            */
  418:           topicId:
  419:             typeof data.topicId ===
  420:               "string" &&
  421:             data.topicId.trim()
  422:               ? data.topicId.trim()
  423:               : quizSnapshot.id,
  424: 
  425:           title:
  426:             typeof data.title ===
  427:               "string" &&
  428:             data.title.trim()
  429:               ? data.title.trim()
  430:               : "AI Generated Quiz",
  431: 
  432:           description:
  433:             typeof data.description ===
  434:               "string" &&
  435:             data.description.trim()
  436:               ? data.description.trim()
  437:               : "Complete this quiz assigned by your teacher.",
  438: 
  439:           estimatedTime:
  440:             typeof data.estimatedTime ===
  441:               "string" &&
  442:             data.estimatedTime.trim()
  443:               ? data.estimatedTime.trim()
  444:               : "10 minutes",
  445: 
  446:           questions,
  447:         };
  448: 
  449:         setGeneratedQuiz(
  450:           loadedQuiz,
  451:         );
  452:       } catch (error) {
  453:         console.error(
  454:           "Failed to load generated quiz:",
  455:           error,
  456:         );
  457: 
  458:         if (!cancelled) {
  459:           setGeneratedQuiz(
  460:             null,
  461:           );
  462: 
  463:           setLoadError(
  464:             "The assigned quiz could not be loaded.",
  465:           );
  466:         }
  467:       } finally {
  468:         if (!cancelled) {
  469:           setLoadingGeneratedQuiz(
  470:             false,
  471:           );
  472:         }
  473:       }
  474:     }
  475: 
  476:     void loadGeneratedQuiz();
  477: 
  478:     return () => {
  479:       cancelled = true;
  480:     };
  481:   }, [
  482:     topicParam,
  483:     qualification,
  484:     examBoard,
  485:     authLoading,
  486:     profileReady,
  487:   ]);
  488: 
  489:   function selectBuiltInQuiz(
  490:     topicId: string,
  491:   ) {
  492:     if (
  493:       !qualification ||
  494:       !examBoard
  495:     ) {
  496:       return;
  497:     }
  498: 
  499:     const quiz =
  500:       getCurriculumQuizByTopic(
  501:         topicId,
  502:         qualification,
  503:         examBoard,
  504:       );
  505: 
  506:     if (!quiz) {
  507:       setLoadError(
  508:         "This quiz is not available for your selected curriculum.",
  509:       );
  510: 
  511:       return;
  512:     }
  513: 
  514:     setSelectedQuizId(
  515:       topicId,
  516:     );
  517: 
  518:     setGeneratedQuiz(null);
  519: 
  520:     setLoadError("");
  521: 
  522:     router.push(
  523:       `/quiz?topic=${encodeURIComponent(
  524:         topicId,
  525:       )}`,
  526:     );
  527:   }
  528: 
  529:   function returnToQuizLibrary() {
  530:     setSelectedQuizId(
  531:       null,
  532:     );
  533: 
  534:     setGeneratedQuiz(null);
  535: 
  536:     setLoadError("");
  537: 
  538:     router.push("/quiz");
  539:   }
  540: 
  541:   /*
  542:    * ------------------------------------------------------------
  543:    * AUTH / CURRICULUM LOADING
  544:    * ------------------------------------------------------------
  545:    */
  546:   if (
  547:     authLoading ||

   12: 
   13: type FirestoreTeacherResource = Omit<
   14:   SavedTeacherResource,
   15:   "id" | "createdAt" | "updatedAt"
   16: > & {
   17:   createdAt?: {
   18:     toDate?: () => Date;
   19:   };
   20:   updatedAt?: {
   21:     toDate?: () => Date;
   22:   };
   23: };
   24: 
   25: function convertTimestamp(
   26:   value:
   27:     | FirestoreTeacherResource["createdAt"]
   28:     | FirestoreTeacherResource["updatedAt"],
   29: ): Date | null {
   30:   if (value && typeof value.toDate === "function") {
   31:     return value.toDate();
   32:   }
   33: 
   34:   return null;
   35: }
   36: 
   37: function formatResourceType(value: string): string {
   38:   return value
   39:     .replace(/-/g, " ")
   40:     .replace(/\b\w/g, (character) => character.toUpperCase());
   41: }
   42: 
   43: function BulletList({
   44:   items,
   45:   emptyMessage = "No information was provided.",
   46: }: {
   47:   items: string[];
   48:   emptyMessage?: string;
   49: }) {
   50:   if (!items || items.length === 0) {
   51:     return <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">{emptyMessage}</p>;
   52:   }
   53: 
   54:   return (
   55:     <ul className="space-y-3">
   56:       {items.map((item, index) => (
   57:         <li
   58:           key={`${item}-${index}`}
   59:           className="flex items-start gap-3 text-sm leading-7 text-slate-700"
   60:         >
   61:           <CheckCircle2 className="mt-1 h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-emerald-500" />
   62: 
   63:           <span>{item}</span>
   64:         </li>
   65:       ))}
   66:     </ul>
   67:   );
   68: }

   74:   children,
   75: }: {

   77:   description?: string;

   94:         </div>

   97:       {children}

  119: export default function StudentResourcePage() {
  120:   const params = useParams<{
  121:     resourceId: string;
  122:   }>();
  123: 
  124:   const { user } = useAuth();
  125: 
  126:   const resourceId = params.resourceId;
  127: 
  128:   const [resource, setResource] = useState<SavedTeacherResource | null>(null);
  129: 
  130:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  131: 
  132:   const [error, setError] = useState("");
  133: 
  134:   const [activeSection, setActiveSection] = useState(0);
  135: 
  136:   const [showAnswers, setShowAnswers] = useState<Record<number, boolean>>({});
  137: 
  138:   const loadResource = useCallback(async () => {
  139:     if (!user?.uid || !resourceId) {
  140:       setResource(null);
  141:       setLoading(false);
  142:       return;
  143:     }
  144: 
  145:     try {
  146:       setLoading(true);
  147:       setError("");
  148: 
  149:       const resourceReference = doc(db, "teacherResources", resourceId);
  150: 
  151:       const snapshot = await getDoc(resourceReference);
  152: 
  153:       if (!snapshot.exists()) {
  154:         setResource(null);
  155:         setError("This teaching resource could not be found.");
  156:         return;
  157:       }
  158: 
  159:       const data = snapshot.data() as FirestoreTeacherResource;
  160: 
  161:       if (data.status !== "published") {
  162:         setResource(null);
  163:         setError("This resource is no longer available to students.");
  164:         return;
  165:       }
  166: 
  167:       setResource({
  168:         id: snapshot.id,
  169:         teacherId: data.teacherId,
  170:         sourceResourceId: data.sourceResourceId,
  171:         title: data.title,
  172:         topic: data.topic,
  173:         resourceType: data.resourceType,
  174:         yearGroup: data.yearGroup,
  175:         examBoard: data.examBoard,
  176:         duration: data.duration,
  177:         difficulty: data.difficulty,
  178:         content: data.content,
  179:         status: data.status,
  180:         createdAt: convertTimestamp(data.createdAt),
  181:         updatedAt: convertTimestamp(data.updatedAt),
  182:       });
  183:     } catch (caughtError) {
  184:       console.error("Unable to load student resource:", caughtError);
  185: 
  186:       setResource(null);
  187: 
  188:       setError(
  189:         caughtError instanceof Error
  190:           ? caughtError.message
  191:           : "The teaching resource could not be loaded.",
  192:       );
  193:     } finally {
  194:       setLoading(false);
  195:     }
  196:   }, [resourceId, user?.uid]);
  197: 
  198:   useEffect(() => {
  199:     void loadResource();
  200:   }, [loadResource]);
  201: 
  202:   const sectionCount = resource?.content.sections.length ?? 0;
  203: 
  204:   const currentSection = useMemo(() => {
  205:     if (!resource || sectionCount === 0) {
  206:       return null;
  207:     }
  208: 
  209:     return resource.content.sections[activeSection];
  210:   }, [activeSection, resource, sectionCount]);
  211: 
  212:   function goToPreviousSection() {
  213:     setActiveSection((current) => Math.max(current - 1, 0));
  214: 
  215:     window.scrollTo({
  216:       top: 0,
  217:       behavior: "smooth",
  218:     });
  219:   }
  220: 
  221:   function goToNextSection() {
  222:     setActiveSection((current) => Math.min(current + 1, sectionCount - 1));
  223: 
  224:     window.scrollTo({
  225:       top: 0,
  226:       behavior: "smooth",
  227:     });
  228:   }
  229: 
  230:   function toggleAnswer(index: number) {
  231:     setShowAnswers((current) => ({
  232:       ...current,
  233:       [index]: !current[index],
  234:     }));
  235:   }
  236: 
  237:   if (loading) {
  238:     return <ResourceSkeleton />;
  239:   }
  240: 
  241:   if (!resource || error) {
  242:     return (
  243:       <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-4xl px-4 py-10 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
  244:         <Link
  245:           href="/assignments"
  246:           className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-bold text-blue-600 transition hover:text-blue-700"
  247:         >
  248:           <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
  249:           Back to assignments
  250:         </Link>
  251: 
  252:         <section className="mt-8 rounded-3xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-8 text-center">
  253:           <div className="mx-auto flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-red-100 text-red-600">
  254:             <AlertCircle className="h-7 w-7" />
  255:           </div>
  256: 
  257:           <h1 className="mt-5 text-2xl font-black text-red-950">
  258:             Resource unavailable
  259:           </h1>
  260: 
  261:           <p className="mx-auto mt-3 max-w-xl text-sm leading-6 text-red-800">
  262:             {error || "This teaching resource could not be found."}
  263:           </p>
  264: 
  265:           <button
  266:             type="button"
  267:             onClick={() => {
  268:               void loadResource();
  269:             }}
  270:             className="mt-6 inline-flex min-h-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-red-600 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-red-700"
  271:           >
  272:             Try again
  273:           </button>
  274:         </section>
  275:       </main>

  277:   }
  278:

  280:

  291:

  387:         </ContentCard>

  389: 
  390:       <div className="grid gap-6 xl:grid-cols-2">
  391:         <ContentCard
  392:           title="Prior knowledge"
  393:           description="What you should already understand"
  394:           icon={<BookOpen className="h-5 w-5" />}
  395:         >
  396:           <BulletList
  397:             items={content.priorKnowledge}
  398:             emptyMessage="No specific prior knowledge is required."
  399:           />
  400:         </ContentCard>
  401:

  403:           title="Key vocabulary"
  404:           description="Important terms for this lesson"
  405:           icon={<Tag className="h-5 w-5" />}
  406:         >

  409:               {content.keywords.map((keyword, index) => (
  410:                 <span
  411:                   key={`${keyword}-${index}`}

  413:                 >
  414:                   {keyword}
  415:                 </span>
  416:               ))}
  417:             </div>

  420:               No key vocabulary was provided.
  421:             </p>

  423:         </ContentCard>

  430:               <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
  431:                 <span className="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-blue-600 font-black text-white">
  432:                   {activeSection + 1}

  448:                 {currentSection.duration}
  449:               </span>
  450:             </div>

  459:               </p>

  466:                 {currentSection.studentTask}
  467:               </p>

  472:                 Check your understanding

  477:               </p>
  478:             </div>

  487:                   items={currentSection.resources}

  489:                 />
  490:               </div>

  496:               type="button"
  497:               onClick={goToPreviousSection}
  498:               disabled={activeSection === 0}
  499:               className="inline-flex min-h-11 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 transition hover:bg-slate-100 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
  500:             >

  502:               Previous section

  504:

  507:                 type="button"
  508:                 onClick={goToNextSection}

  548:                   Correct understanding
  549:                 </h4>
  550: 
  551:                 <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-7 text-slate-700">
  552:                   {item.correction}
  553:                 </p>
  554:               </article>
  555:             ))}
  556:           </div>
  557:         ) : (
  558:           <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">

  565:         id="assessment"
  566:         className="scroll-mt-6 rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm"

  569:           <div className="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-violet-50 text-violet-600">
  570:             <FileQuestion className="h-5 w-5" />
  571:           </div>
  572: 
  573:           <div>
  574:             <h2 className="text-xl font-black text-slate-950">
  575:               Assessment questions
  576:             </h2>
  577: 
  578:             <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
  579:               Attempt each question before revealing the model answer.
  580:             </p>
  581:           </div>
  582:         </div>
  583: 
  584:         {content.assessmentQuestions.length > 0 ? (
  585:           <div className="mt-6 space-y-5">
  586:             {content.assessmentQuestions.map((question, index) => {
  587:               const answerVisible = Boolean(showAnswers[index]);
  588: 
  589:               return (
  590:                 <article
  591:                   key={`${question.question}-${index}`}
  592:                   className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-200"
  593:                 >
  594:                   <div className="bg-slate-50 p-5">
  595:                     <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
  596:                       <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
  597:                         <span className="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-violet-600 text-sm font-black text-white">
  598:                           {index + 1}
  599:                         </span>
  600: 
  601:                         <p className="text-sm font-bold leading-7 text-slate-950">
  602:                           {question.question}
  603:                         </p>
  604:                       </div>
  605:

  607:                         {question.marks}{" "}
  608:                         {question.marks === 1 ? "mark" : "marks"}
  609:                       </span>
  610:                     </div>
  611:                   </div>
  612:

  614:                     <button
  615:                       type="button"
  616:                       onClick={() => toggleAnswer(index)}
  617:                       className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-violet-200 bg-violet-50 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-bold text-violet-700 transition hover:bg-violet-100"
  618:                     >
  619:                       {answerVisible
  620:                         ? "Hide model answer"
  621:                         : "Reveal model answer"}
  622:                     </button>
  623: 
  624:                     {answerVisible && (
  625:                       <div className="mt-4 rounded-2xl border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 p-5">
  626:                         <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wide text-emerald-700">
  627:                           Model answer
  628:                         </p>
  629: 
  630:                         <p className="mt-2 whitespace-pre-line text-sm leading-7 text-slate-700">
  631:                           {question.answer}
  632:                         </p>
  633:                       </div>
  634:                     )}
  635:                   </div>
  636:                 </article>
  637:               );
  638:             })}
  639:           </div>
  640:         ) : (
  641:           <p className="mt-5 text-sm text-slate-500">
  642:             No assessment questions were provided.
  643:           </p>
  644:         )}
  645:       </section>
  646: 
  647:       <div className="grid gap-6 xl:grid-cols-2">
  648:         <ContentCard
  649:           title="Homework"
  650:           description="Complete this after the lesson"
  651:           icon={<GraduationCap className="h-5 w-5" />}
  652:         >
  653:           <p className="whitespace-pre-line text-sm leading-7 text-slate-700">
  654:             {content.homework || "No homework was provided."}
  655:           </p>
  656:         </ContentCard>
  657: 
  658:         <ContentCard
  659:           title="Final reminder"
  660:           description="Before returning to your assignment"
  661:           icon={<Lightbulb className="h-5 w-5" />}
  662:         >
  663:           <p className="text-sm leading-7 text-slate-700">
  664:             Check that you have completed each task and attempted the assessment
  665:             questions. Return to the assignment page when you are ready to mark
  666:             the work complete.
  667:           </p>
  668:         </ContentCard>
  669:       </div>
  670: 
  671:       <section className="rounded-3xl border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 p-6 text-center">
  672:         <CheckCircle2 className="mx-auto h-10 w-10 text-blue-600" />
  673: 
  674:         <h2 className="mt-3 text-xl font-black text-blue-950">
  675:           Finished the resource?
  676:         </h2>
  677: 
  678:         <p className="mx-auto mt-2 max-w-xl text-sm leading-6 text-blue-800">
  679:           Return to your assignment and mark it as completed after finishing all
  680:           required work.
  681:         </p>
  682: 
  683:         <Link
  684:           href="/assignments"
  685:           className="mt-5 inline-flex min-h-12 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-blue-700"
  686:         >
  687:           Return to assignments
  688:           <ChevronRight className="h-4 w-4" />
  689:         </Link>
  690:       </section>
  691:     </main>
  692:   );
  693: }

   37: function defaultAcademicYear(): string {
   38:   const now = new Date();
   39: 
   40:   const year =
   41:     now.getFullYear();
   42: 
   43:   /*
   44:    * Treat August onward as preparation for the new UK academic year.
   45:    */
   46:   const startYear =
   47:     now.getMonth() >= 7
   48:       ? year
   49:       : year - 1;
   50: 
   51:   return `${startYear}/${startYear + 1}`;
   52: }
   53: 
   54: function qualificationLabel(
   55:   qualification:
   56:     Qualification | "",
   57: ): string {
   58:   if (
   59:     qualification ===
   60:     "A_LEVEL"
   61:   ) {
   62:     return "A Level";
   63:   }
   64: 
   65:   return qualification || "Not set";
   66: }
   67:

   75:

   85:     UnifiedTeacherAssignment[]

  154:   function loadClasses() {
  155:     if (!user?.uid) {
  156:       return Promise.resolve().then(() => {
  157:         setClasses([]);
  158:         setUnifiedAssignments([]);
  159:         setLoadingClasses(false);
  160:       });
  161:     }
  162: 
  163:     const teacherId = user.uid;
  164: 
  165:     return Promise.resolve()
  166:       .then(() => {
  167:         setLoadingClasses(true);
  168:

  170:           getTeacherClasses(

  173:           getUnifiedTeacherAssignments(
  174:             teacherId,
  175:           ),
  176:         ]);
  177:       })
  178:       .then(([
  179:         loaded,

  181:       ]) => {
  182:         setClasses(
  183:           loaded,
  184:         );
  185: 
  186:         setUnifiedAssignments(
  187:           assignmentSummary.assignments,
  188:         );
  189:       })
  190:       .catch((error) => {
  191:         console.error(
  192:           "Failed to load classes:",
  193:           error,
  194:         );
  195: 
  196:         toast.error(
  197:           "Could not load your classes.",
  198:         );
  199:       })
  200:       .finally(() => {
  201:         setLoadingClasses(
  202:           false,
  203:         );
  204:       });
  205:   }
  206: 
  207:   useEffect(() => {
  208:     if (
  209:       authLoading ||
  210:       !profileReady
  211:     ) {
  212:       return;
  213:     }
  214: 
  215:     void loadClasses();
  216:   }, [
  217:     authLoading,
  218:     profileReady,
  219:     user?.uid,
  220:   ]);
  221: 
  222:   const visibleClasses =
  223:     useMemo(() => {
  224:       const search =
  225:         searchTerm
  226:           .trim()
  227:           .toLowerCase();
  228: 
  229:       return classes.filter(
  230:         (item) => {
  231:           const matchesStatus =
  232:             statusFilter ===
  233:               "all" ||
  234:             item.status ===
  235:               statusFilter;
  236: 
  237:           if (
  238:             !matchesStatus
  239:           ) {
  240:             return false;
  241:           }
  242: 
  243:           if (!search) {
  244:             return true;
  245:           }
  246: 
  247:           return [
  248:             item.name,
  249:             item.subject,
  250:             item.yearGroup,
  251:             item.academicYear,
  252:             item.qualification,
  253:             item.examBoard,
  254:           ].some(
  255:             (value) =>
  256:               value
  257:                 .toLowerCase()
  258:                 .includes(
  259:                   search,
  260:                 ),
  261:           );
  262:         },
  263:       );
  264:     }, [
  265:       classes,
  266:       searchTerm,
  267:       statusFilter,
  268:     ]);
  269: 
  270:   const activeClasses =
  271:     classes.filter(
  272:       (item) =>
  273:         item.status ===
  274:         "active",
  275:     );
  276: 
  277:   const totalStudents =
  278:     activeClasses.reduce(
  279:       (
  280:         total,
  281:         item,
  282:       ) =>
  283:         total +
  284:         item.studentIds.length,
  285:       0,
  286:     );
  287: 
  288:   const activeClassIds =
  289:     new Set(
  290:       activeClasses.map(
  291:         (item) => item.id,
  292:       ),
  293:     );
  294: 
  295:   const totalAssignments =
  296:     unifiedAssignments.filter(
  297:       (assignment) =>
  298:         activeClassIds.has(
  299:           assignment.classId,
  300:         ),
  301:     ).length;
  302: 
  303:   const assignmentCountByClass =
  304:     useMemo(() => {
  305:       const counts =
  306:         new Map<string, number>();
  307: 
  308:       unifiedAssignments.forEach(
  309:         (assignment) => {
  310:           counts.set(
  311:             assignment.classId,
  312:             (counts.get(
  313:               assignment.classId,
  314:             ) || 0) + 1,
  315:           );
  316:         },
  317:       );
  318:

  320:     }, [
  321:       unifiedAssignments,
  322:     ]);
  323: 
  324:   async function handleCreateClass(
  325:     event:
  326:       FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>,
  327:   ) {
  328:     event.preventDefault();
  329: 
  330:     if (!user?.uid) {

  332:         "You must be logged in as a teacher.",
  333:       );
  334:       return;
  335:     }
  336: 
  337:     if (
  338:       !profile?.schoolId
  339:     ) {
  340:       toast.error(
  341:         "Create or join your school organisation before creating classes.",
  342:       );
  343:       return;
  344:     }
  345:

  365:

  367:

  373:

  377:           user.uid,
  378:

  380:           profile?.name ||
  381:           user.displayName ||
  382:           user.email ||
  383:           "Teacher",
  384: 
  385:         schoolId:
  386:           profile.schoolId,
  387:       });
  388: 
  389:       toast.success(
  390:         "Class created successfully.",
  391:       );
  392: 
  393:       setClassName("");
  394:       setYearGroup("");
  395:       setSubject(
  396:         "Computer Science",
  397:       );
  398:       setAcademicYear(
  399:         defaultAcademicYear(),
  400:       );
  401:       setQualification(
  402:         "GCSE",
  403:       );
  404:       setExamBoard(
  405:         "OCR",
  406:       );
  407:       setShowForm(false);
  408: 
  409:       await loadClasses();

  827: 
  828: function Metric({
  829:   label,
  830:   value,
  831: }: {
  832:   label: string;
  833:   value: number;
  834: }) {
  835:   return (
  836:     <Card>
  837:       <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-500">
  838:         {label}
  839:       </p>
  840: 
  841:       <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-black text-slate-950">
  842:         {value}
  843:       </p>
  844:     </Card>
  845:   );
  846: }
  847: 
  848: function MiniMetric({
  849:   label,
  850:   value,
  851: }: {
  852:   label: string;
  853:   value: number;
  854: }) {
  855:   return (
  856:     <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 p-4">
  857:       <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
  858:         {label}
  859:       </p>
  860: 
  861:       <p className="mt-1 text-xl font-black text-slate-900">
  862:         {value}
  863:       </p>
  864:     </div>
  865:   );
  866: }
  867: 
  868: function Field({
  869:   label,
  870:   value,
  871:   onChange,
  872:   placeholder,
  873: }: {
  874:   label: string;
  875:   value: string;
  876:   onChange: (
  877:     value: string,
  878:   ) => void;
  879:   placeholder: string;
  880: }) {
  881:   return (
  882:     <label>
  883:       <span className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-700">
  884:         {label}
  885:       </span>
  886: 
  887:       <input
  888:         type="text"
  889:         value={value}
  890:         onChange={(
  891:           event,
  892:         ) =>
  893:           onChange(
  894:             event.target.value,
  895:           )
  896:         }
  897:         placeholder={
  898:           placeholder
  899:         }
  900:         className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3"
  901:       />
  902:     </label>
  903:   );
  904: }

    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import Link from "next/link";
    4: import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
    5: import {
    6:   Archive,
    7:   BookOpen,
    8:   CheckCircle2,
    9:   Copy,
   10:   FileQuestion,
   11:   Filter,
   12:   Loader2,
   13:   RefreshCcw,
   14:   Search,
   15:   Send,
   16:   Sparkles,
   17:   Trash2,
   18: } from "lucide-react";
   19: import toast from "react-hot-toast";
   20: 
   21: import Card from "@/components/ui/Card";
   22: import Skeleton from "@/components/ui/Skeleton";
   23: import { useAuth } from "@/contexts/AuthContext";
   24: import {
   25:   deleteTeacherContent,
   26:   duplicateTeacherContent,
   27:   getTeacherContentLibrary,
   28:   teacherContentKindLabel,
   29:   updateTeacherContentLifecycle,
   30: } from "@/services/teacherContentLibraryService";
   31: import type {
   32:   TeacherContentItem,
   33:   TeacherContentKind,
   34:   TeacherContentLifecycle,
   35: } from "@/types/teacherContent";
   36: 
   37: type KindFilter = "all" | TeacherContentKind;
   38: type LifecycleFilter = "all" | TeacherContentLifecycle;
   39: 
   40: function formatQualification(value: string): string {
   41:   return value === "A_LEVEL" ? "A Level" : value || "Not set";
   42: }
   43: 
   44: function formatDate(value: Date | null): string {
   45:   if (!value) return "Recently";
   46:   return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", {
   47:     day: "numeric",
   48:     month: "short",
   49:     year: "numeric",
   50:   }).format(value);
   51: }
   52: 
   53: function lifecycleTone(value: TeacherContentLifecycle): string {
   54:   if (value === "published") return "border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700";
   55:   if (value === "archived") return "border-slate-200 bg-slate-100 text-slate-600";
   56:   return "border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 text-amber-700";
   57: }
   58: 
   59: function kindIcon(kind: TeacherContentKind): ReactNode {
   60:   if (kind === "teaching-resource") return <BookOpen className="h-4 w-4" />;
   61:   if (kind === "ai-quiz") return <Sparkles className="h-4 w-4" />;
   62:   return <FileQuestion className="h-4 w-4" />;
   63: }
   64: 
   65: export default function TeacherContentHubPage() {
   66:   const { user, loading: authLoading, profileReady } = useAuth();
   67:   const [items, setItems] = useState<TeacherContentItem[]>([]);
   68:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   69:   const [error, setError] = useState("");
   70:   const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState("");
   71:   const [kindFilter, setKindFilter] = useState<KindFilter>("all");
   72:   const [lifecycleFilter, setLifecycleFilter] = useState<LifecycleFilter>("all");
   73:   const [qualificationFilter, setQualificationFilter] = useState("all");
   74:   const [examBoardFilter, setExamBoardFilter] = useState("all");
   75:   const [updatingKey, setUpdatingKey] = useState("");
   76: 
   77:   const load = useCallback(async () => {
   78:     const teacherId = user?.uid;
   79:     if (!teacherId) {
   80:       setItems([]);
   81:       setLoading(false);
   82:       return;
   83:     }
   84: 
   85:     try {
   86:       setLoading(true);
   87:       setError("");
   88:       setItems(await getTeacherContentLibrary(teacherId));
   89:     } catch (caughtError) {
   90:       console.error("Unable to load teacher content library:", caughtError);
   91:       setError(
   92:         caughtError instanceof Error
   93:           ? caughtError.message
   94:           : "The Content Hub could not be loaded.",
   95:       );
   96:     } finally {
   97:       setLoading(false);
   98:     }
   99:   }, [user?.uid]);
  100: 
  101:   useEffect(() => {
  102:     if (authLoading || !profileReady) return;
  103:     void load();
  104:   }, [authLoading, profileReady, load]);
  105: 
  106:   const qualifications = useMemo(
  107:     () => Array.from(new Set(items.map((item) => item.qualification).filter(Boolean))).sort(),
  108:     [items],
  109:   );
  110:   const examBoards = useMemo(
  111:     () => Array.from(new Set(items.map((item) => item.examBoard).filter(Boolean))).sort(),
  112:     [items],
  113:   );
  114: 
  115:   const filteredItems = useMemo(() => {
  116:     const search = searchTerm.trim().toLowerCase();
  117:     return items.filter((item) => {
  118:       const matchesSearch =
  119:         !search ||
  120:         [
  121:           item.title,
  122:           item.description,
  123:           item.topic,
  124:           item.examBoard,
  125:           item.yearGroup,
  126:           teacherContentKindLabel(item.kind),
  127:         ]
  128:           .join(" ")
  129:           .toLowerCase()
  130:           .includes(search);
  131:       return (
  132:         matchesSearch &&
  133:         (kindFilter === "all" || item.kind === kindFilter) &&
  134:         (lifecycleFilter === "all" || item.lifecycle === lifecycleFilter) &&
  135:         (qualificationFilter === "all" || item.qualification === qualificationFilter) &&
  136:         (examBoardFilter === "all" || item.examBoard === examBoardFilter)
  137:       );
  138:     });
  139:   }, [items, searchTerm, kindFilter, lifecycleFilter, qualificationFilter, examBoardFilter]);
  140: 
  141:   const counts = useMemo(
  142:     () => ({
  143:       total: items.length,
  144:       draft: items.filter((item) => item.lifecycle === "draft").length,
  145:       published: items.filter((item) => item.lifecycle === "published").length,
  146:       archived: items.filter((item) => item.lifecycle === "archived").length,
  147:     }),
  148:     [items],
  149:   );
  150: 
  151:   async function changeLifecycle(item: TeacherContentItem, lifecycle: TeacherContentLifecycle) {
  152:     try {
  153:       setUpdatingKey(item.key);
  154:       await updateTeacherContentLifecycle(item, lifecycle);
  155:       toast.success(
  156:         lifecycle === "published"
  157:           ? "Content published."
  158:           : lifecycle === "archived"
  159:             ? "Content archived."
  160:             : "Content returned to draft.",
  161:       );
  162:       await load();
  163:     } catch (caughtError) {
  164:       toast.error(caughtError instanceof Error ? caughtError.message : "Content status could not be changed.");
  165:     } finally {
  166:       setUpdatingKey("");
  167:     }
  168:   }
  169: 
  170:   async function duplicate(item: TeacherContentItem) {
  171:     const teacherId = user?.uid;
  172:     if (!teacherId) return;
  173:     try {
  174:       setUpdatingKey(item.key);
  175:       await duplicateTeacherContent(item, teacherId);
  176:       toast.success("Draft copy created.");
  177:       await load();
  178:     } catch (caughtError) {
  179:       toast.error(caughtError instanceof Error ? caughtError.message : "The content could not be duplicated.");
  180:     } finally {
  181:       setUpdatingKey("");
  182:     }
  183:   }
  184: 
  185:   async function remove(item: TeacherContentItem) {
  186:     if (item.lifecycle !== "archived") {
  187:       toast.error("Archive content before deleting it.");
  188:       return;
  189:     }
  190:     if (!window.confirm(`Permanently delete "${item.title}"? This cannot be undone.`)) return;
  191:     try {
  192:       setUpdatingKey(item.key);
  193:       await deleteTeacherContent(item);
  194:       toast.success("Archived content deleted.");
  195:       await load();
  196:     } catch (caughtError) {
  197:       toast.error(caughtError instanceof Error ? caughtError.message : "The content could not be deleted.");
  198:     } finally {
  199:       setUpdatingKey("");
  200:     }
  201:   }
  202: 
  203:   function clearFilters() {
  204:     setSearchTerm("");
  205:     setKindFilter("all");
  206:     setLifecycleFilter("all");
  207:     setQualificationFilter("all");
  208:     setExamBoardFilter("all");
  209:   }
  210: 
  211:   if (authLoading || !profileReady || loading) {

  213:       <div className="space-y-6">
  214:         <Skeleton className="h-52 rounded-3xl" />
  215:         <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">

  217:             <Skeleton key={index} className="h-28 rounded-3xl" />
  218:           ))}
  219:         </div>
  220:         <Skeleton className="h-96 rounded-3xl" />
  221:       </div>
  222:     );
  223:   }
  224: 
  225:   return (
  226:     <div className="space-y-7">
  227:       <section className="rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-800 via-violet-800 to-fuchsia-700 p-7 text-white shadow-xl">

  229:           <div>
  230:             <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-violet-100">T1F Â· Teacher Content</p>

  232:             <p className="mt-3 max-w-3xl text-sm leading-7 text-violet-100">

  240:           </div>
  241:         </div>
  242:       </section>
  243: 
  244:       <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
  245:         <Summary label="All content" value={counts.total} />
  246:         <Summary label="Draft" value={counts.draft} />
  247:         <Summary label="Published" value={counts.published} />
  248:         <Summary label="Archived" value={counts.archived} />
  249:       </div>
  250: 
  251:       <Card className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 p-6">
  252:         <div className="grid gap-3 xl:grid-cols-[1.6fr_repeat(4,minmax(150px,auto))_auto]">
  253:           <label className="relative">
  254:             <span className="sr-only">Search teacher content</span>
  255:             <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute left-4 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
  256:             <input value={searchTerm} onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)} placeholder="Search title, topic, board or description..." className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 py-3 pl-11 pr-4 text-sm" />
  257:           </label>
  258:           <FilterSelect value={kindFilter} onChange={(v) => setKindFilter(v as KindFilter)}>
  259:             <option value="all">All types</option><option value="teaching-resource">Teaching resources</option><option value="ai-quiz">AI quizzes</option><option value="exam-paper">Exam question sets</option>
  260:           </FilterSelect>
  261:           <FilterSelect value={lifecycleFilter} onChange={(v) => setLifecycleFilter(v as LifecycleFilter)}>
  262:             <option value="all">All statuses</option><option value="draft">Draft</option><option value="published">Published</option><option value="archived">Archived</option>
  263:           </FilterSelect>

  265:             <option value="all">All qualifications</option>{qualifications.map((v) => <option key={v} value={v}>{formatQualification(v)}</option>)}
  266:           </FilterSelect>

  269:           </FilterSelect>

  271:         </div>
  272:         <p className="mt-4 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-500">Showing <span className="font-black text-slate-900">{filteredItems.length}</span> of <span className="font-black text-slate-900">{items.length}</span></p>
  273:       </Card>
  274: 
  275:       {error ? (
  276:         <Card className="border border-red-200 bg-red-50"><p className="font-black text-red-800">Content Hub unavailable</p><p className="mt-2 text-sm text-red-700">{error}</p></Card>
  277:       ) : filteredItems.length === 0 ? (
  278:         <Card className="rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 p-12 text-center"><BookOpen className="mx-auto h-10 w-10 text-slate-300" /><h2 className="mt-4 text-xl font-black text-slate-950">No content matches</h2><p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">Create content or change the filters above.</p></Card>
  279:       ) : (
  280:         <section className="grid gap-5 xl:grid-cols-2">
  281:           {filteredItems.map((item) => {
  282:             const busy = updatingKey === item.key;
  283:             return (
  284:               <article key={item.key} className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
  285:                 <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">

  287:                   <span className={`rounded-full border px-3 py-1 text-xs font-black capitalize ${lifecycleTone(item.lifecycle)}`}>{item.lifecycle}</span>
  288:                   {item.examBoard && <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-black text-slate-600">{item.examBoard}</span>}
  289:                   {item.qualification && <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-black text-slate-600">{formatQualification(item.qualification)}</span>}
  290:                 </div>
  291:                 <h2 className="mt-4 text-xl font-black text-slate-950">{item.title}</h2>
  292:                 <p className="mt-2 text-sm font-bold text-indigo-700">{item.topic || "Topic not tagged"}</p>
  293:                 <p className="mt-2 line-clamp-3 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">{item.description}</p>
  294:                 <div className="mt-5 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-4">
  295:                   <MiniMetric label="Questions" value={item.questionCount ?? "â€”"} />
  296:                   <MiniMetric label="Marks" value={item.totalMarks ?? "â€”"} />
  297:                   <MiniMetric label="Difficulty" value={item.difficulty || "â€”"} />
  298:                   <MiniMetric label="Updated" value={formatDate(item.updatedAt || item.createdAt)} />
  299:                 </div>
  300:                 <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
  301:                   <Link href={item.openHref} className="rounded-xl bg-slate-950 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-black text-white">Open</Link>
  302:                   {item.assignHref && <Link href={item.assignHref} className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-black text-white"><Send className="h-4 w-4" />Assign</Link>}
  303:                   <button type="button" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void duplicate(item)} className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-black text-slate-700 disabled:opacity-50"><Copy className="h-4 w-4" />Duplicate</button>
  304:                   {item.lifecycle === "draft" ? (
  305:                     <button type="button" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void changeLifecycle(item, "published")} className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-black text-emerald-700 disabled:opacity-50"><CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4" />Publish</button>
  306:                   ) : item.lifecycle === "published" ? (
  307:                     <>
  308:                       <button type="button" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void changeLifecycle(item, "draft")} className="rounded-xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-black text-amber-700 disabled:opacity-50">Return to draft</button>
  309:                       <button type="button" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void changeLifecycle(item, "archived")} className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-slate-100 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-black text-slate-700 disabled:opacity-50"><Archive className="h-4 w-4" />Archive</button>
  310:                     </>
  311:                   ) : (
  312:                     <>
  313:                       <button type="button" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void changeLifecycle(item, "draft")} className="rounded-xl border border-indigo-200 bg-indigo-50 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-black text-indigo-700 disabled:opacity-50">Restore draft</button>
  314:                       <button type="button" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void remove(item)} className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-black text-red-700 disabled:opacity-50">{busy ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />}Delete</button>
  315:                     </>
  316:                   )}
  317:                 </div>
  318:               </article>
  319:             );
  320:           })}
  321:         </section>
  322:       )}
  323: 
  324:       <Card className="rounded-3xl border border-cyan-200 bg-cyan-50 p-6">
  325:         <p className="font-black text-cyan-950">Built-in curriculum content</p>
  326:         <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-cyan-900">CS Master lessons and programming challenges remain platform content. They are assignable through the Assignment Wizard but are not archived or deleted from a teacher-owned library.</p>
  327:         <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
  328:           <Link href="/teacher/assignment-wizard" className="rounded-xl bg-cyan-700 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-black text-white">Assign built-in content</Link>
  329:           <Link href="/teacher/resources" className="rounded-xl border border-cyan-300 bg-white px-4 py-3 text-sm font-black text-cyan-800">Resource Library</Link>
  330:           <Link href="/teacher/quiz-library" className="rounded-xl border border-cyan-300 bg-white px-4 py-3 text-sm font-black text-cyan-800">Quiz Library</Link>
  331:           <Link href="/teacher/question-bank" className="rounded-xl border border-cyan-300 bg-white px-4 py-3 text-sm font-black text-cyan-800">Question Bank</Link>
  332:         </div>
  333:       </Card>
  334:     </div>
  335:   );
  336: }
  337: 
  338: function Summary({ label, value }: { label: string; value: number }) {
  339:   return <Card className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 p-5"><p className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-500">{label}</p><p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-black text-slate-950">{value}</p></Card>;
  340: }
  341: 
  342: function MiniMetric({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string | number }) {
  343:   return <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 p-3"><p className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-400">{label}</p><p className="mt-1 truncate text-sm font-black capitalize text-slate-900">{value}</p></div>;
  344: }
  345: 
  346: function FilterSelect({ value, onChange, children }: { value: string; onChange: (value: string) => void; children: ReactNode }) {
  347:   return (
  348:     <label className="relative">
  349:       <span className="sr-only">Content filter</span>
  350:       <Filter className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
  351:       <select value={value} onChange={(event) => onChange(event.target.value)} className="w-full appearance-none rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white py-3 pl-9 pr-4 text-sm">{children}</select>
  352:     </label>
  353:   );
  354: }

   57:   const [error, setError] = useState("");
   58: 
   59:   async function load() {
   60:     const teacherId =
   61:       user?.uid;
   62: 
   63:     if (!teacherId) {
   64:       setLoading(false);
   65:       return;
   66:     }
   67: 
   68:     setLoading(true);
   69:     setError("");

   71:     try {

   73:         nextCandidates,

   82:       ]);

   85:         nextCandidates,
   86:       );
   87: 
   88:       setInterventions(
   89:         nextInterventions,
   90:       );
   91: 
   92:       setSelectedImpactId(
   93:         (current) => {
   94:           if (!current) {
   95:             return null;
   96:           }
   97: 
   98:           return nextInterventions.some(
   99:             (item) =>
  100:               item.id === current,
  101:           )
  102:             ? current
  103:             : null;
  104:         },
  105:       );
  106:     } catch (caughtError: unknown) {
  107:       const firebaseCode =
  108:         typeof caughtError === "object" &&
  109:         caughtError !== null &&
  110:         "code" in caughtError &&
  111:         typeof (caughtError as { code?: unknown }).code === "string"
  112:           ? (caughtError as { code: string }).code
  113:           : "";
  114: 
  115:       setCandidates([]);
  116:       setInterventions([]);
  117: 
  118:       setError(
  119:         firebaseCode === "permission-denied" ||
  120:           firebaseCode === "firestore/permission-denied"
  121:           ? "Intervention data is unavailable for this teacher account."
  122:           : caughtError instanceof Error
  123:             ? caughtError.message
  124:             : "Intervention data could not be loaded.",
  125:       );
  126:     } finally {
  127:       setLoading(false);
  128:     }
  129:   }
  130: 
  131:   useEffect(() => {
  132:     void load();

  394:             interventions.map((intervention) => {
  395:               const reviewing = selectedImpactId === intervention.id;
  396: 
  397:               return (
  398:                 <article
  399:                   key={intervention.id}
  400:                   className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-5 transition hover:border-teal-300"

  403:                     {intervention.status}
  404:                   </span>
  405: 
  406:                   <h3 className="mt-4 font-black">
  407:                     {intervention.studentName}
  408:                   </h3>
  409: 
  410:                   <p className="font-bold text-teal-700">
  411:                     {intervention.topic}
  412:                   </p>
  413: 
  414:                   <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-slate-600">
  415:                     {
  416:                       intervention.steps.filter(
  417:                         (step) => step.status === "completed",
  418:                       ).length
  419:                     }
  420:                     /{intervention.steps.length} steps
  421:                   </p>
  422: 
  423:                   <div className="mt-5 grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2">
  424:                     <Link
  425:                       href={`/teacher/interventions/${intervention.id}`}
  426:                       className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-4 text-xs font-black text-slate-700 transition hover:bg-slate-50"
  427:                     >
  428:                       Open
  429:                       <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />
  430:                     </Link>
  431: 
  432:                     <button
  433:                       type="button"
  434:                       onClick={() =>
  435:                         setSelectedImpactId((current) =>
  436:                           current === intervention.id
  437:                             ? null
  438:                             : intervention.id,
  439:                         )
  440:                       }
  441:                       className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-violet-600 px-4 text-xs font-black text-white transition hover:bg-violet-700"
  442:                     >
  443:                       <Activity className="h-4 w-4" />
  444:                       {reviewing ? "Hide impact" : "Review impact"}
  445:                     </button>
  446:                   </div>
  447:                 </article>
  448:               );
  449:             })
  450:           ) : (
  451:             <p className="text-slate-500">
  452:               No interventions yet.
  453:             </p>
  454:           )}
  455:         </div>
  456:       </Card>
  457: 
  458:       {selectedImpactId && user?.uid && (
  459:         <InterventionImpactCard
  460:           interventionId={selectedImpactId}
  461:           teacherId={user.uid}
  462:         />
  463:       )}
  464: 
  465:       <CreateInterventionModal
  466:         candidate={selected}
  467:         teacherId={user?.uid || ""}
  468:         teacherName={profile?.name || "Teacher"}
  469:         onClose={() => setSelected(null)}
  470:         onCreated={() => {
  471:           setSelected(null);
  472:           void load();
  473:         }}
  474:       />
  475:     </div>
  476:   );
  477: }

  250: export default function EditTeacherResourcePage() {
  251:   const params = useParams<{
  252:     resourceId: string;
  253:   }>();
  254: 
  255:   const router = useRouter();
  256:   const resourceId = params.resourceId;
  257: 
  258:   const [currentUser, setCurrentUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  259: 
  260:   const [authLoading, setAuthLoading] = useState(true);
  261: 
  262:   const [resource, setResource] = useState<GeneratedTeachingResource | null>(
  263:     null,
  264:   );
  265: 
  266:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  267: 
  268:   const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  269: 
  270:   const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  271: 
  272:   const [success, setSuccess] = useState<string | null>(null);
  273: 
  274:   useEffect(() => {
  275:     const unsubscribe = onAuthStateChanged(auth, (user) => {
  276:       setCurrentUser(user);
  277:       setAuthLoading(false);
  278:     });
  279: 
  280:     return unsubscribe;
  281:   }, []);
  282: 
  283:   const loadResource = useCallback(async () => {
  284:     if (!currentUser || !resourceId) {
  285:       setLoading(false);
  286:       return;
  287:     }
  288: 
  289:     setLoading(true);
  290:     setError(null);
  291: 
  292:     try {
  293:       const resourceReference = doc(db, "teacherResources", resourceId);
  294: 
  295:       const resourceSnapshot = await getDoc(resourceReference);
  296: 
  297:       if (!resourceSnapshot.exists()) {
  298:         setError("This teaching resource could not be found.");
  299:         setResource(null);
  300:         return;
  301:       }
  302: 
  303:       const data = resourceSnapshot.data() as EditableResourceDocument;
  304: 
  305:       if (data.teacherId !== currentUser.uid) {
  306:         setError("You do not have permission to edit this resource.");
  307:         setResource(null);
  308:         return;
  309:       }
  310: 
  311:       setResource(data.content);
  312:     } catch (caughtError) {
  313:       console.error("Failed to load editable resource:", caughtError);
  314: 
  315:       setError(
  316:         caughtError instanceof Error
  317:           ? caughtError.message
  318:           : "The resource could not be loaded.",
  319:       );
  320:     } finally {
  321:       setLoading(false);
  322:     }
  323:   }, [currentUser, resourceId]);
  324: 
  325:   useEffect(() => {
  326:     if (!authLoading) {
  327:       void loadResource();
  328:     }
  329:   }, [authLoading, loadResource]);
  330: 
  331:   function updateTopLevelField<K extends keyof GeneratedTeachingResource>(
  332:     field: K,
  333:     value: GeneratedTeachingResource[K],
  334:   ) {
  335:     setResource((current) => {
  336:       if (!current) {
  337:         return current;
  338:       }
  339: 
  340:       return {
  341:         ...current,
  342:         [field]: value,
  343:       };
  344:     });
  345: 
  346:     setError(null);
  347:     setSuccess(null);
  348:   }
  349: 
  350:   function updateStringArrayItem(
  351:     field: StringArrayField,
  352:     index: number,
  353:     value: string,
  354:   ) {
  355:     setResource((current) => {
  356:       if (!current) {
  357:         return current;
  358:       }

  361:       updatedItems[index] = value;
  362: 
  363:       return {

  366:       };
  367:     });
