  564: 
  565:   function updateAssessmentQuestion(
  566:     index: number,
  567:     field: keyof ResourceAssessmentQuestion,
  568:     value: string | number,
  569:   ) {
  570:     setResource((current) => {
  571:       if (!current) {
  572:         return current;
  573:       }
  574: 
  575:       const assessmentQuestions = [...current.assessmentQuestions];
  576: 
  577:       assessmentQuestions[index] = {
  578:         ...assessmentQuestions[index],
  579:         [field]: value,
  580:       };
  581: 
  582:       return {
  583:         ...current,
  584:         assessmentQuestions,
  585:       };
  586:     });
  587:   }
  588: 
  589:   function addAssessmentQuestion() {
  590:     setResource((current) => {
  591:       if (!current) {
  592:         return current;
  593:       }
  594: 
  595:       return {
  596:         ...current,
  597:         assessmentQuestions: [
  598:           ...current.assessmentQuestions,
  599:           createEmptyAssessmentQuestion(),
  600:         ],
  601:       };
  602:     });
  603:   }
  604: 
  605:   function removeAssessmentQuestion(index: number) {
  606:     setResource((current) => {
  607:       if (!current || current.assessmentQuestions.length <= 1) {
  608:         return current;
  609:       }
  610: 
  611:       return {
  612:         ...current,
  613:         assessmentQuestions: current.assessmentQuestions.filter(
  614:           (_, itemIndex) => itemIndex !== index,
  615:         ),
  616:       };
  617:     });
  618:   }
  619: 
  620:   function validateResource(): string | null {
  621:     if (!resource) {
  622:       return "The resource could not be loaded.";
  623:     }
  624: 
  625:     if (resource.title.trim().length < 2) {
  626:       return "Enter a valid resource title.";
  627:     }
  628: 
  629:     if (resource.topic.trim().length < 2) {
  630:       return "Enter a valid topic.";
  631:     }
  632: 
  633:     if (!resource.overview.trim()) {
  634:       return "Enter a resource overview.";
  635:     }
  636: 
  637:     if (resource.learningObjectives.some((item) => !item.trim())) {
  638:       return "Learning objectives cannot contain blank items.";
  639:     }
  640: 
  641:     if (resource.successCriteria.some((item) => !item.trim())) {
  642:       return "Success criteria cannot contain blank items.";
  643:     }
  644: 
  645:     if (
  646:       resource.sections.some(
  647:         (section) =>
  648:           !section.title.trim() ||
  649:           !section.teacherInstructions.trim() ||
  650:           !section.studentTask.trim(),
  651:       )
  652:     ) {
  653:       return "Every section requires a title, teacher instructions and student task.";
  654:     }
  655: 
  656:     if (
  657:       resource.assessmentQuestions.some(
  658:         (question) =>
  659:           !question.question.trim() ||
  660:           !question.answer.trim() ||
  661:           question.marks < 1,
  662:       )
  663:     ) {
  664:       return "Every assessment question requires a question, answer and valid mark value.";
  665:     }
  666: 
  667:     return null;
  668:   }
  669: 
  670:   async function handleSave() {
  671:     const validationError = validateResource();
  672: 
  673:     if (validationError) {
  674:       setError(validationError);
  675:       setSuccess(null);
  676:       return;
  677:     }
  678: 
  679:     if (!resource) {
  680:       return;
  681:     }
  682: 
  683:     setSaving(true);
  684:     setError(null);
  685:     setSuccess(null);
  686: 
  687:     try {
  688:       const cleanedResource: GeneratedTeachingResource = {
  689:         ...resource,
  690:         title: resource.title.trim(),
  691:         topic: resource.topic.trim(),
  692:         overview: resource.overview.trim(),
  693:         learningObjectives: resource.learningObjectives.map((item) =>
  694:           item.trim(),
  695:         ),
  696:         successCriteria: resource.successCriteria.map((item) => item.trim()),
  697:         keywords: resource.keywords.map((item) => item.trim()).filter(Boolean),
  698:         priorKnowledge: resource.priorKnowledge
  699:           .map((item) => item.trim())
  700:           .filter(Boolean),
  701:         sections: resource.sections.map((section) => ({
  702:           ...section,
  703:           title: section.title.trim(),
  704:           duration: section.duration.trim(),
  705:           teacherInstructions: section.teacherInstructions.trim(),
  706:           studentTask: section.studentTask.trim(),
  707:           assessment: section.assessment.trim(),
  708:           resources: section.resources
  709:             .map((item) => item.trim())
  710:             .filter(Boolean),
  711:         })),
  712:         differentiation: {
  713:           support: resource.differentiation.support
  714:             .map((item) => item.trim())
  715:             .filter(Boolean),
  716:           core: resource.differentiation.core
  717:             .map((item) => item.trim())
  718:             .filter(Boolean),
  719:           stretch: resource.differentiation.stretch
  720:             .map((item) => item.trim())
  721:             .filter(Boolean),
  722:         },
  723:         misconceptions: resource.misconceptions
  724:           .map((item) => ({
  725:             misconception: item.misconception.trim(),
  726:             correction: item.correction.trim(),
  727:           }))
  728:           .filter((item) => item.misconception && item.correction),
  729:         assessmentQuestions: resource.assessmentQuestions.map((question) => ({
  730:           question: question.question.trim(),
  731:           answer: question.answer.trim(),
  732:           marks: Math.max(1, Math.round(question.marks)),
  733:         })),
  734:         homework: resource.homework.trim(),
  735:         teacherNotes: resource.teacherNotes.trim(),
  736:       };
  737: 
  738:       await updateTeacherResourceContent(resourceId, cleanedResource);
  739: 
  740:       setResource(cleanedResource);
  741:       setSuccess("Your resource changes were saved successfully.");
  742: 
  743:       window.setTimeout(() => {
  744:         router.push(`/teacher/resources/${resourceId}`);
  745:       }, 800);
  746:     } catch (caughtError) {
  747:       console.error("Failed to save resource changes:", caughtError);
  748: 
  749:       setError(
  750:         caughtError instanceof Error
  751:           ? caughtError.message
  752:           : "Your changes could not be saved.",
  753:       );
  754:     } finally {
  755:       setSaving(false);
  756:     }
  757:   }
  758: 
  759:   if (authLoading || loading) {
  760:     return <EditPageSkeleton />;
  761:   }
  762: 
  763:   if (error && !resource) {
  764:     return (
  765:       <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-4xl px-4 py-10 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
  766:         <Link
  767:           href="/teacher/resources"
  768:           className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-bold text-indigo-600 hover:text-indigo-700"
  769:         >
  770:           <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
  771:           Back to Resource Library
  772:         </Link>
  773: 
  774:         <section className="mt-8 rounded-3xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-8 text-center">
  775:           <AlertCircle className="mx-auto h-10 w-10 text-red-600" />
  776: 
  777:           <h1 className="mt-4 text-2xl font-bold text-red-950">
  778:             Resource unavailable
  779:           </h1>
  780: 
  781:           <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-red-800">{error}</p>
  782:         </section>
  783:       </main>
  784:     );
  785:   }
  786: 
  787:   if (!resource) {
  788:     return null;
  789:   }
  790: 
  791:   return (
  792:     <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl space-y-6 px-4 py-6 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
  793:       <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
  794:         <Link
  795:           href={`/teacher/resources/${resourceId}`}
  796:           className="inline-flex w-fit items-center gap-2 text-sm font-bold text-indigo-600 hover:text-indigo-700"
  797:         >
  798:           <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
  799:           Cancel editing
  800:         </Link>
  801: 
  802:         <button
  803:           type="button"
  804:           onClick={handleSave}
  805:           disabled={saving}
  806:           className="inline-flex w-fit items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white shadow-lg shadow-indigo-100 transition hover:bg-indigo-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-slate-300 disabled:shadow-none"
  807:         >
  808:           {saving ? (
  809:             <>
  810:               <Loader2 className="h-5 w-5 animate-spin" />
  811:               Saving changes...
  812:             </>
  813:           ) : (
  814:             <>
  815:               <Save className="h-5 w-5" />
  816:               Save changes
  817:             </>
  818:           )}
  819:         </button>
  820:       </div>
  821: 
  822:       <section className="rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-700 via-indigo-600 to-sky-600 p-6 text-white shadow-lg sm:p-8">
  823:         <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
  824:           <div className="flex h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-white/15">
  825:             <BookOpen className="h-6 w-6" />
  826:           </div>
  827: 
  828:           <div>
  829:             <h1 className="text-3xl font-extrabold tracking-tight">
  830:               Edit teaching resource
  831:             </h1>
  832: 
  833:             <p className="mt-2 max-w-2xl leading-7 text-indigo-50">
  834:               Refine the generated lesson content before publishing, exporting
  835:               or assigning it to a class.
  836:             </p>
  837:           </div>
  838:         </div>
  839:       </section>
  840: 
  841:       {error && (
  842:         <div
  843:           role="alert"
  844:           className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4 text-red-800"
  845:         >
  846:           <AlertCircle className="mt-0.5 h-5 w-5 shrink-0" />
  847: 
  848:           <div>
  849:             <p className="font-bold">Changes not saved</p>
  850: 
  851:             <p className="mt-1 text-sm leading-6">{error}</p>
  852:           </div>
  853:         </div>
  854:       )}
  855: 
  856:       {success && (
  857:         <div
  858:           role="status"
  859:           className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 p-4 text-emerald-800"
  860:         >
  861:           <CheckCircle2 className="mt-0.5 h-5 w-5 shrink-0" />
  862: 
  863:           <p className="text-sm font-semibold">{success}</p>
  864:         </div>
  865:       )}
  866: 
  867:       <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm sm:p-6">
  868:         <SectionHeader
  869:           icon={<BookOpen className="h-5 w-5" />}
  870:           title="Resource details"
  871:           description="Update the resource title, topic and curriculum information."
  872:         />
  873: 
  874:         <div className="grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-2">
  875:           <div>
  876:             <FieldLabel htmlFor="resource-title" required>
  877:               Resource title
  878:             </FieldLabel>
  879: 
  880:             <input
  881:               id="resource-title"
  882:               value={resource.title}
  883:               onChange={(event) =>
  884:                 updateTopLevelField("title", event.target.value)
  885:               }
  886:               maxLength={200}
  887:               className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-4 py-3 text-sm text-slate-900 outline-none transition focus:border-indigo-500 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-indigo-100"
  888:             />
  889:           </div>
  890: 
  891:           <div>
  892:             <FieldLabel htmlFor="resource-topic" required>
  893:               Topic
  894:             </FieldLabel>
  895: 
  896:             <input
  897:               id="resource-topic"
  898:               value={resource.topic}
  899:               onChange={(event) =>
  900:                 updateTopLevelField("topic", event.target.value)
  901:               }
  902:               maxLength={150}
  903:               className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-4 py-3 text-sm text-slate-900 outline-none transition focus:border-indigo-500 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-indigo-100"
  904:             />
  905:           </div>
  906: 
  907:           <div>
  908:             <FieldLabel htmlFor="resource-year">
  909:               Year group or qualification
  910:             </FieldLabel>
  911: 
  912:             <div className="relative">
  913:               <select
  914:                 id="resource-year"
  915:                 value={resource.yearGroup}
  916:                 onChange={(event) =>
  917:                   updateTopLevelField("yearGroup", event.target.value)
  918:                 }
  919:                 className="w-full appearance-none rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-4 py-3 pr-10 text-sm text-slate-900 outline-none transition focus:border-indigo-500 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-indigo-100"
  920:               >
  921:                 {YEAR_GROUPS.map((yearGroup) => (
  922:                   <option key={yearGroup} value={yearGroup}>
  923:                     {yearGroup}
  924:                   </option>
  925:                 ))}
  926:               </select>
  927: 
  928:               <ChevronDown className="pointer-events-none absolute right-3 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
  929:             </div>
  930:           </div>
  931: 
  932:           <div>
  933:             <FieldLabel htmlFor="resource-board">Exam board</FieldLabel>
  934: 
  935:             <div className="relative">
  936:               <select
  937:                 id="resource-board"
  938:                 value={resource.examBoard}
  939:                 onChange={(event) =>
  940:                   updateTopLevelField("examBoard", event.target.value)
  941:                 }
  942:                 className="w-full appearance-none rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-4 py-3 pr-10 text-sm text-slate-900 outline-none transition focus:border-indigo-500 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-indigo-100"
  943:               >
  944:                 {EXAM_BOARDS.map((examBoard) => (
  945:                   <option key={examBoard} value={examBoard}>
  946:                     {examBoard}
  947:                   </option>
  948:                 ))}
  949:               </select>
  950: 
  951:               <ChevronDown className="pointer-events-none absolute right-3 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
  952:             </div>
  953:           </div>
  954: 
  955:           <div>
  956:             <FieldLabel htmlFor="resource-duration">Duration</FieldLabel>
  957: 
  958:             <div className="relative">
  959:               <Clock3 className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
  960: 
  961:               <input
  962:                 id="resource-duration"
  963:                 value={resource.duration}
  964:                 onChange={(event) =>
  965:                   updateTopLevelField("duration", event.target.value)
  966:                 }
  967:                 placeholder="For example: 60 minutes"
  968:                 className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white py-3 pl-10 pr-4 text-sm text-slate-900 outline-none transition focus:border-indigo-500 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-indigo-100"
  969:               />
  970:             </div>
  971:           </div>
  972: 
  973:           <div>
  974:             <FieldLabel htmlFor="resource-difficulty">Difficulty</FieldLabel>
  975: 
  976:             <select
  977:               id="resource-difficulty"
  978:               value={resource.difficulty}
  979:               onChange={(event) =>
  980:                 updateTopLevelField("difficulty", event.target.value)
  981:               }
  982:               className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-4 py-3 text-sm capitalize text-slate-900 outline-none transition focus:border-indigo-500 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-indigo-100"
  983:             >
  984:               {DIFFICULTY_LEVELS.map((difficulty) => (
  985:                 <option key={difficulty} value={difficulty}>
  986:                   {difficulty}
  987:                 </option>
  988:               ))}
  989:             </select>
  990:           </div>
  991:         </div>
  992: 
  993:         <div className="mt-5">
  994:           <FieldLabel htmlFor="resource-overview" required>
  995:             Resource overview
  996:           </FieldLabel>
  997: 
  998:           <textarea
  999:             id="resource-overview"
 1000:             value={resource.overview}
 1001:             onChange={(event) =>
 1002:               updateTopLevelField("overview", event.target.value)
 1003:             }
 1004:             rows={5}
 1005:             maxLength={2500}
 1006:             className="w-full resize-y rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-4 py-3 text-sm leading-7 text-slate-900 outline-none transition focus:border-indigo-500 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-indigo-100"
 1007:           />
 1008:         </div>
 1009:       </section>
 1010: 
 1011:       <div className="grid gap-6 xl:grid-cols-2">
 1012:         <EditableStringList
 1013:           title="Learning objectives"
 1014:           description="What students should know or be able to do."
 1015:           icon={<Target className="h-5 w-5" />}
 1016:           items={resource.learningObjectives}
 1017:           placeholder="Enter a measurable learning objective."
 1018:           onChange={(index, value) =>
 1019:             updateStringArrayItem("learningObjectives", index, value)
 1020:           }
 1021:           onAdd={() => addStringArrayItem("learningObjectives")}
 1022:           onRemove={(index) =>
 1023:             removeStringArrayItem("learningObjectives", index)
 1024:           }
 1025:         />
 1026: 
 1027:         <EditableStringList
 1028:           title="Success criteria"
 1029:           description="How students will demonstrate successful learning."
 1030:           icon={<CheckCircle2 className="h-5 w-5" />}
 1031:           items={resource.successCriteria}
 1032:           placeholder="Enter a success criterion."
 1033:           onChange={(index, value) =>
 1034:             updateStringArrayItem("successCriteria", index, value)
 1035:           }
 1036:           onAdd={() => addStringArrayItem("successCriteria")}
 1037:           onRemove={(index) => removeStringArrayItem("successCriteria", index)}
 1038:         />
 1039: 
 1040:         <EditableStringList
 1041:           title="Prior knowledge"
 1042:           description="Knowledge students should already possess."
 1043:           icon={<BookOpen className="h-5 w-5" />}
 1044:           items={
 1045:             resource.priorKnowledge.length > 0 ? resource.priorKnowledge : [""]
 1046:           }
 1047:           placeholder="Enter required prior knowledge."
 1048:           onChange={(index, value) =>
 1049:             updateStringArrayItem("priorKnowledge", index, value)
 1050:           }
 1051:           onAdd={() => addStringArrayItem("priorKnowledge")}
 1052:           onRemove={(index) => removeStringArrayItem("priorKnowledge", index)}
 1053:         />
 1054: 
 1055:         <EditableStringList
 1056:           title="Keywords"
 1057:           description="Important vocabulary students should use."
 1058:           icon={<Tag className="h-5 w-5" />}
 1059:           items={resource.keywords.length > 0 ? resource.keywords : [""]}
 1060:           placeholder="Enter a keyword."
 1061:           onChange={(index, value) =>
 1062:             updateStringArrayItem("keywords", index, value)
 1063:           }
 1064:           onAdd={() => addStringArrayItem("keywords")}
 1065:           onRemove={(index) => removeStringArrayItem("keywords", index)}
 1066:         />
 1067:       </div>
 1068: 
 1069:       <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm sm:p-6">
 1070:         <SectionHeader
 1071:           icon={<BookOpen className="h-5 w-5" />}
 1072:           title="Resource sequence"
 1073:           description="Edit teacher instructions, student tasks, assessment opportunities and required resources."
 1074:         />
 1075: 
 1076:         <div className="space-y-6">
 1077:           {resource.sections.map((section, index) => (
 1078:             <article
 1079:               key={`section-${index}`}
 1080:               className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-200"
 1081:             >
 1082:               <header className="flex flex-col gap-4 border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
 1083:                 <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
 1084:                   <span className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-sm font-bold text-white">
 1085:                     {index + 1}
 1086:                   </span>
 1087: 
 1088:                   <p className="font-bold text-slate-950">
 1089:                     Section {index + 1}
 1090:                   </p>
 1091:                 </div>
 1092: 
 1093:                 <button
 1094:                   type="button"
 1095:                   onClick={() => removeSection(index)}
 1096:                   disabled={resource.sections.length === 1}
 1097:                   className="inline-flex w-fit items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-red-600 transition hover:bg-red-100 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
 1098:                 >
 1099:                   <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
 1100:                   Remove section
 1101:                 </button>
 1102:               </header>
 1103: 
 1104:               <div className="grid gap-5 p-5 lg:grid-cols-2">
 1105:                 <div>
 1106:                   <FieldLabel htmlFor={`section-title-${index}`} required>
 1107:                     Section title
 1108:                   </FieldLabel>
 1109: 
 1110:                   <input
 1111:                     id={`section-title-${index}`}
 1112:                     value={section.title}
 1113:                     onChange={(event) =>
 1114:                       updateSection(index, "title", event.target.value)
 1115:                     }
 1116:                     className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none transition focus:border-indigo-500 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-indigo-100"
 1117:                   />
 1118:                 </div>
 1119: 
 1120:                 <div>
 1121:                   <FieldLabel htmlFor={`section-duration-${index}`}>
 1122:                     Duration
 1123:                   </FieldLabel>
 1124: 
 1125:                   <input
 1126:                     id={`section-duration-${index}`}
 1127:                     value={section.duration}
 1128:                     onChange={(event) =>
 1129:                       updateSection(index, "duration", event.target.value)
 1130:                     }
 1131:                     className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none transition focus:border-indigo-500 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-indigo-100"
 1132:                   />
 1133:                 </div>
 1134: 
 1135:                 <div>
 1136:                   <FieldLabel
 1137:                     htmlFor={`teacher-instructions-${index}`}
 1138:                     required
 1139:                   >
 1140:                     Teacher instructions
 1141:                   </FieldLabel>
 1142: 
 1143:                   <textarea
 1144:                     id={`teacher-instructions-${index}`}
 1145:                     value={section.teacherInstructions}
 1146:                     onChange={(event) =>
 1147:                       updateSection(
 1148:                         index,
 1149:                         "teacherInstructions",
 1150:                         event.target.value,
 1151:                       )
 1152:                     }
 1153:                     rows={6}
 1154:                     className="w-full resize-y rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm leading-7 outline-none transition focus:border-indigo-500 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-indigo-100"
 1155:                   />
 1156:                 </div>
 1157: 
 1158:                 <div>
 1159:                   <FieldLabel htmlFor={`student-task-${index}`} required>
 1160:                     Student task
 1161:                   </FieldLabel>
 1162: 
 1163:                   <textarea

FILE: components\adaptive\ReviewSchedule.tsx
====================================================================================================

    1: import type { AdaptiveTopicMastery } from "@/types/adaptiveLearning";
    2: 
    3: export default function ReviewSchedule({
    4:   topics,
    5: }: {
    6:   topics: AdaptiveTopicMastery[];
    7: }) {
    8:   const upcoming = [...topics]
    9:     .sort(
   10:       (first, second) =>
   11:         first.nextReviewAt.getTime() - second.nextReviewAt.getTime(),
   12:     )
   13:     .slice(0, 6);
   14: 
   15:   return (
   16:     <div className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6">
   17:       <p className="text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-teal-600">
   18:         Spaced retrieval
   19:       </p>
   20:       <h2 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">
   21:         Review schedule
   22:       </h2>
   23: 
   24:       <div className="mt-5 space-y-3">
   25:         {upcoming.map((topic) => {
   26:           const due = topic.nextReviewAt.getTime() <= Date.now();
   27: 
   28:           return (
   29:             <div
   30:               key={topic.id}
   31:               className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 p-4"
   32:             >
   33:               <div>
   34:                 <p className="font-black text-slate-900">{topic.topic}</p>
   35:                 <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
   36:                   Every {topic.reviewIntervalDays} day
   37:                   {topic.reviewIntervalDays === 1 ? "" : "s"}
   38:                 </p>
   39:               </div>
   40: 
   41:               <span
   42:                 className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold ${
   43:                   due ? "bg-red-100 text-red-700" : "bg-teal-100 text-teal-700"
   44:                 }`}
   45:               >
   46:                 {due
   47:                   ? "Due now"
   48:                   : topic.nextReviewAt.toLocaleDateString("en-GB")}
   49:               </span>
   50:             </div>
   51:           );
   52:         })}
   53:       </div>
   54:     </div>
   55:   );
   56: }

    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
    4: import Link from "next/link";

    6: import Card from "@/components/ui/Card";

    9:   examTrainerQuestionBank,
   10:   examTrainerTopics,
   11: } from "@/data/examTrainerQuestionBank";

   13:   createExamTrainerAttempt,
   14:   createExamTrainerAttemptId,

   17:   submitExamTrainerAttempt,

   19: import {
   20:   buildExamQuestions,
   21:   markExamTrainerAttempt,

   23: import type { ExamTrainerAttempt, ExamTrainerDifficulty, ExamTrainerReport } from "@/types/examTrainer";
   24: 
   25: type Stage = "loading" | "setup" | "exam" | "marking" | "report";
   26: 
   27: function formatSeconds(totalSeconds: number): string {
   28:   const minutes = Math.floor(totalSeconds / 60);
   29:   const seconds = totalSeconds % 60;
   30:   return `${minutes}:${seconds.toString().padStart(2, "0")}`;
   31: }
   32: 
   33: export default function ExamQuestionTrainer() {
   34:   const { user, profile } = useAuth();
   35: 
   36:   const [stage, setStage] = useState<Stage>("loading");
   37:   const [topic, setTopic] = useState("all");
   38:   const [difficulty, setDifficulty] = useState<ExamTrainerDifficulty | "all">(
   39:     "all",
   40:   );
   41:   const [questionCount, setQuestionCount] = useState(6);
   42:   const [attempt, setAttempt] = useState<ExamTrainerAttempt | null>(null);
   43:   const [report, setReport] = useState<ExamTrainerReport | null>(null);
   44:   const [resumeAttempt, setResumeAttempt] = useState<ExamTrainerAttempt | null>(
   45:     null,
   46:   );
   47:   const [error, setError] = useState("");
   48: 
   49:   const submittingRef = useRef(false);
   50:   const lastSavedSignatureRef = useRef("");
   51: 
   52:   const questions = attempt?.questions ?? [];
   53:   const answers = attempt?.answers ?? [];
   54:   const currentIndex = attempt?.currentQuestionIndex ?? 0;
   55:   const secondsRemaining = attempt?.secondsRemaining ?? 0;
   56:   const currentQuestion = questions[currentIndex];
   57: 
   58:   const availableCount = useMemo(
   59:     () =>
   60:       examTrainerQuestionBank.filter((question) => {
   61:         const topicMatches = topic === "all" || question.topic === topic;
   62:         const difficultyMatches =
   63:           difficulty === "all" || question.difficulty === difficulty;
   64: 
   65:         return topicMatches && difficultyMatches;
   66:       }).length,
   67:     [difficulty, topic],
   68:   );
   69: 
   70:   useEffect(() => {
   71:     if (!user) {
   72:       setStage("setup");
   73:       return;
   74:     }
   75: 
   76:     const userId = user.uid;
   77:     let active = true;
   78: 
   79:     async function loadResumeAttempt() {
   80:       try {
   81:         const existing = await getLatestInProgressExamTrainerAttempt(userId);
   82: 
   83:         if (active && existing) {
   84:           setResumeAttempt(existing);
   85:         }
   86:       } catch (loadError) {
   87:         console.error("Resume attempt load error:", loadError);
   88:       } finally {
   89:         if (active) {
   90:           setStage("setup");
   91:         }
   92:       }
   93:     }
   94: 
   95:     void loadResumeAttempt();
   96: 
   97:     return () => {
   98:       active = false;
   99:     };
  100:   }, [user]);
  101: 
  102:   useEffect(() => {
  103:     if (stage !== "exam" || !attempt) return;
  104: 
  105:     if (attempt.secondsRemaining <= 0) {
  106:       void submitExam();
  107:       return;
  108:     }
  109: 
  110:     const timer = window.setInterval(() => {
  111:       setAttempt((current) =>
  112:         current
  113:           ? {
  114:               ...current,
  115:               secondsRemaining: Math.max(0, current.secondsRemaining - 1),
  116:               updatedAt: new Date(),
  117:             }
  118:           : current,
  119:       );
  120:     }, 1000);
  121: 
  122:     return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  123:   }, [attempt?.id, attempt?.secondsRemaining, stage]);
  124: 
  125:   useEffect(() => {
  126:     if (stage !== "exam" || !attempt) return;
  127: 
  128:     const signature = JSON.stringify({
  129:       id: attempt.id,
  130:       answers: attempt.answers,
  131:       currentQuestionIndex: attempt.currentQuestionIndex,
  132:       secondsRemaining: Math.floor(attempt.secondsRemaining / 5),
  133:     });
  134: 
  135:     if (signature === lastSavedSignatureRef.current) return;
  136: 
  137:     const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
  138:       void saveExamTrainerDraft(attempt)
  139:         .then(() => {
  140:           lastSavedSignatureRef.current = signature;
  141:         })
  142:         .catch((saveError) => {
  143:           console.error("Exam trainer autosave error:", saveError);
  144:         });
  145:     }, 700);
  146: 
  147:     return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
  148:   }, [attempt, stage]);
  149: 
  150:   function updateAttempt(
  151:     updater: (current: ExamTrainerAttempt) => ExamTrainerAttempt,
  152:   ) {
  153:     setAttempt((current) => (current ? updater(current) : current));
  154:   }
  155: 
  156:   async function startExam() {
  157:     if (!user) {
  158:       setError("Please sign in before starting an exam.");
  159:       return;
  160:     }
  161: 
  162:     const selectedQuestions = buildExamQuestions({
  163:       topic,
  164:       difficulty,
  165:       count: questionCount,
  166:       questionBank: examTrainerQuestionBank,
  167:     });
  168: 
  169:     if (selectedQuestions.length === 0) {
  170:       setError("No questions match the selected options.");
  171:       return;
  172:     }
  173: 
  174:     const startedAt = new Date();
  175:     const durationSeconds = Math.max(10, selectedQuestions.length * 3) * 60;
  176: 
  177:     const newAttempt: ExamTrainerAttempt = {
  178:       id: createExamTrainerAttemptId(user.uid, startedAt),
  179:       studentId: user.uid,
  180:       selectedTopic: topic,
  181:       selectedDifficulty: difficulty,
  182:       requestedQuestionCount: questionCount,
  183:       questions: selectedQuestions,
  184:       answers: selectedQuestions.map((question) => ({
  185:         questionId: question.id,
  186:         response: "",
  187:       })),
  188:       currentQuestionIndex: 0,
  189:       durationSeconds,
  190:       secondsRemaining: durationSeconds,
  191:       status: "in_progress",
  192:       report: null,
  193:       startedAt,
  194:       updatedAt: startedAt,
  195:       submittedAt: null,
  196:     };
  197: 
  198:     try {
  199:       await createExamTrainerAttempt(newAttempt);
  200:       setAttempt(newAttempt);
  201:       setResumeAttempt(null);
  202:       setReport(null);
  203:       setError("");
  204:       setStage("exam");
  205:     } catch (createError) {
  206:       console.error("Exam attempt creation error:", createError);
  207:       setError("The exam could not be created or saved.");
  208:     }
  209:   }
  210: 
  211:   function resumeSavedAttempt() {
  212:     if (!resumeAttempt) return;
  213: 
  214:     setAttempt(resumeAttempt);
  215:     setTopic(resumeAttempt.selectedTopic);
  216:     setDifficulty(resumeAttempt.selectedDifficulty);
  217:     setQuestionCount(resumeAttempt.requestedQuestionCount);
  218:     setError("");
  219:     setStage("exam");
  220:   }
  221: 
  222:   function updateAnswer(response: string) {
  223:     if (!currentQuestion) return;
  224: 
  225:     updateAttempt((current) => ({
  226:       ...current,
  227:       answers: current.answers.map((answer) =>
  228:         answer.questionId === currentQuestion.id
  229:           ? { ...answer, response }
  230:           : answer,
  231:       ),
  232:       updatedAt: new Date(),
  233:     }));
  234:   }
  235: 
  236:   function moveToQuestion(nextIndex: number) {
  237:     updateAttempt((current) => ({
  238:       ...current,
  239:       currentQuestionIndex: Math.max(
  240:         0,
  241:         Math.min(current.questions.length - 1, nextIndex),
  242:       ),
  243:       updatedAt: new Date(),
  244:     }));
  245:   }
  246: 
  247:   async function submitExam() {
  248:     if (!attempt || submittingRef.current) return;
  249: 
  250:     submittingRef.current = true;
  251: 
  252:     try {
  253:       setStage("marking");
  254:       setError("");
  255: 
  256:       const result = await markExamTrainerAttempt({
  257:         questions: attempt.questions,
  258:         answers: attempt.answers,
  259:         qualification: profile?.qualification,
  260:       });
  261: 
  262:       await submitExamTrainerAttempt({
  263:         attempt,
  264:         report: result,
  265:       });
  266: 
  267:       setAttempt({
  268:         ...attempt,
  269:         status: "submitted",
  270:         report: result,
  271:         submittedAt: new Date(),
  272:         updatedAt: new Date(),
  273:       });
  274:       setReport(result);
  275:       setStage("report");
  276:     } catch (submissionError) {
  277:       console.error("Exam trainer submission error:", submissionError);
  278:       setError(
  279:         submissionError instanceof Error
  280:           ? submissionError.message
  281:           : "The exam could not be marked and saved.",
  282:       );
  283:       setStage("exam");
  284:     } finally {
  285:       submittingRef.current = false;
  286:     }
  287:   }
  288: 
  289:   const currentResponse =
  290:     answers.find((answer) => answer.questionId === currentQuestion?.id)
  291:       ?.response ?? "";
  292: 
  293:   if (stage === "loading") {
  294:     return (
  295:       <Card>
  296:         <p className="py-20 text-center font-bold text-slate-500">
  297:           Preparing Exam Trainer...
  298:         </p>
  299:       </Card>
  300:     );
  301:   }
  302: 
  303:   if (stage === "setup") {
  304:     return (
  305:       <div className="space-y-8">
  306:         <Card className="border-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-slate-950 via-blue-950 to-indigo-900 text-white">
  307:           <p className="text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-blue-200">
  308:             GCSE Exam Centre
  309:           </p>
  310:           <h1 className="mt-3 text-4xl font-black">Exam Question Trainer</h1>
  311:           <p className="mt-4 max-w-3xl leading-7 text-blue-100">
  312:             Build a timed paper. Answers are autosaved and submitted results
  313:             update your learning evidence.
  314:           </p>
  315: 
  316:           <Link

    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
    4: import { Menu } from "lucide-react";
    5: import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
    6: 
    7: import Sidebar from "@/components/layout/Sidebar";
    8: import StudentAccessGate from "@/components/student/StudentAccessGate";
    9: 
   10: export default function AppShell({
   11:   children,
   12: }: {
   13:   children: React.ReactNode;
   14: }) {
   15:   const pathname = usePathname();
   16: 
   17:   const [mobileSidebarOpen, setMobileSidebarOpen] =
   18:     useState(false);
   19: 
   20:   const publicPages = [
   21:     "/",
   22:     "/landing",
   23:     "/login",
   24:     "/register",
   25:     "/forgot-password",
   26:     "/onboarding",
   27: 
   28:     /*
   29:      * Teacher applicants use this route before receiving
   30:      * the teacher role, so it must not render the normal
   31:      * authenticated sidebar shell.
   32:      */
   33:     "/teacher-access",
   34:   ];
   35: 
   36:   const isPublicPage =
   37:     publicPages.includes(pathname);
   38: 
   39:   const isTeacherWorkspace =
   40:     pathname === "/teacher" ||
   41:     pathname.startsWith("/teacher/");
   42: 
   43:   const isAdminWorkspace =
   44:     pathname === "/admin" ||
   45:     pathname.startsWith("/admin/");
   46: 
   47:   useEffect(() => {
   48:     setMobileSidebarOpen(false);
   49:   }, [pathname]);
   50: 
   51:   useEffect(() => {
   52:     if (!mobileSidebarOpen) {
   53:       return;
   54:     }
   55: 
   56:     const previousOverflow =
   57:       document.body.style.overflow;
   58: 
   59:     document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
   60: 
   61:     return () => {
   62:       document.body.style.overflow =
   63:         previousOverflow;
   64:     };
   65:   }, [mobileSidebarOpen]);
   66: 
   67:   if (isPublicPage) {
   68:     return (
   69:       <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-100">
   70:         {children}
   71:       </main>
   72:     );
   73:   }
   74: 
   75:   const applicationShell = (
   76:     <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-100 xl:flex">
   77:       <Sidebar
   78:         mobileOpen={mobileSidebarOpen}
   79:         onMobileClose={() =>
   80:           setMobileSidebarOpen(false)
   81:         }
   82:       />
   83: 
   84:       <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
   85:         <header className="sticky top-0 z-30 flex h-16 items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 shadow-sm sm:px-6 xl:hidden">
   86:           <div>
   87:             <p className="text-sm font-black tracking-wide text-slate-950">
   88:               CS MASTER
   89:             </p>
   90: 
   91:             <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
   92:               Learning Platform
   93:             </p>
   94:           </div>
   95: 
   96:           <button
   97:             type="button"
   98:             onClick={() =>
   99:               setMobileSidebarOpen(true)
  100:             }
  101:             className="inline-flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-slate-800 transition hover:bg-slate-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-4 focus:ring-blue-100"
  102:             aria-label="Open navigation"
  103:             aria-expanded={mobileSidebarOpen}
  104:             aria-controls="mobile-navigation"
  105:           >
  106:             <Menu className="h-5 w-5" />
  107:           </button>
  108:         </header>
  109: 
  110:         <main className="min-w-0 p-3 sm:p-4 md:p-6">
  111:           {children}
  112:         </main>
  113:       </div>
  114:     </div>
  115:   );
  116: 
  117:   /*
  118:    * Teacher and administrator workspaces keep their own
  119:    * role-specific protection. Every other authenticated
  120:    * application route is a student workspace and passes
  121:    * through the student production gate.
  122:    */
  123:   if (
  124:     isTeacherWorkspace ||
  125:     isAdminWorkspace
  126:   ) {
  127:     return applicationShell;
  128:   }
  129: 
  130:   return (
  131:     <StudentAccessGate>
  132:       {applicationShell}
  133:     </StudentAccessGate>
  134:   );
  135: }

FILE: components\lesson-engine\LessonPracticeStep.tsx
====================================================================================================

    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
    4: 
    5: import ReadAloudButton from "@/components/audio/ReadAloudButton";
    6: import type { PracticeQuestion } from "@/types/curriculum";
    7: import type { PracticeResponse } from "@/types/interactiveLesson";
    8: 
    9: type Props = {
   10:   questions: PracticeQuestion[];
   11:   initialResponses: PracticeResponse[];
   12:   onChange: (responses: PracticeResponse[]) => void;
   13: };
   14: 
   15: function normaliseAnswer(value: string): string {
   16:   return value
   17:     .trim()
   18:     .toLowerCase()
   19:     .replace(/[.,!?]/g, "")
   20:     .replace(/[-_]/g, " ")
   21:     .replace(/\s+/g, " ");
   22: }
   23: 
   24: function isAcceptedAnswer(
   25:   response: string,
   26:   question: PracticeQuestion,
   27: ): boolean {
   28:   const normalisedResponse = normaliseAnswer(response);
   29: 
   30:   const acceptedAnswers = [
   31:     question.answer,
   32:     ...(question.acceptedAnswers ?? []),
   33:   ].map(normaliseAnswer);
   34: 
   35:   return acceptedAnswers.includes(normalisedResponse);
   36: }
   37: 
   38: function createInitialResponses(
   39:   questions: PracticeQuestion[],
   40:   existingResponses: PracticeResponse[],
   41: ): PracticeResponse[] {
   42:   return questions.map((_, index) => {
   43:     const existingResponse = existingResponses.find(
   44:       (response) => response.questionIndex === index,
   45:     );
   46: 
   47:     return (
   48:       existingResponse ?? {
   49:         questionIndex: index,
   50:         response: "",
   51:         correct: false,
   52:         checked: false,
   53:       }
   54:     );
   55:   });
   56: }
   57: 
   58: export default function LessonPracticeStep({
   59:   questions,
   60:   initialResponses,
   61:   onChange,
   62: }: Props) {
   63:   const [responses, setResponses] = useState<PracticeResponse[]>(() =>
   64:     createInitialResponses(questions, initialResponses),
   65:   );
   66: 
   67:   useEffect(() => {
   68:     setResponses(createInitialResponses(questions, initialResponses));
   69:   }, [questions, initialResponses]);
   70: 
   71:   function updateResponse(
   72:     questionIndex: number,
   73:     updates: Partial<PracticeResponse>,
   74:   ) {
   75:     const updatedResponses = responses.map((response) =>
   76:       response.questionIndex === questionIndex
   77:         ? {
   78:             ...response,
   79:             ...updates,
   80:           }
   81:         : response,
   82:     );
   83: 
   84:     setResponses(updatedResponses);
   85:     onChange(updatedResponses);
   86:   }
   87: 
   88:   function checkAnswer(questionIndex: number) {
   89:     const question = questions[questionIndex];
   90: 
   91:     const response = responses.find(
   92:       (item) => item.questionIndex === questionIndex,
   93:     );
   94: 
   95:     if (!question || !response) {
   96:       return;
   97:     }
   98: 
   99:     updateResponse(questionIndex, {
  100:       checked: true,
  101:       correct: isAcceptedAnswer(response.response, question),
  102:     });
  103:   }
  104: 
  105:   if (questions.length === 0) {
  106:     return (
  107:       <div className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6">
  108:         <p className="italic text-slate-500">
  109:           No guided-practice questions are required for this lesson.
  110:         </p>
  111:       </div>
  112:     );
  113:   }
  114: 
  115:   return (
  116:     <div className="space-y-5">
  117:       {questions.map((question, index) => {
  118:         const response = responses.find(
  119:           (item) => item.questionIndex === index,
  120:         ) ?? {
  121:           questionIndex: index,
  122:           response: "",
  123:           correct: false,
  124:           checked: false,
  125:         };
  126: 
  127:         return (
  128:           <article
  129:             key={`${question.question}-${index}`}
  130:             className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm"
  131:           >
  132:             <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-start sm:justify-between">
  133:               <div>
  134:                 <p className="text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-blue-600">
  135:                   Question {index + 1}
  136:                 </p>
  137: 
  138:                 <h3 className="mt-2 text-lg font-black text-slate-950">
  139:                   {question.question}
  140:                 </h3>
  141:               </div>
  142: 
  143:               <ReadAloudButton text={question.question} label="Read question" />
  144:             </div>
  145: 
  146:             {question.hint && (
  147:               <details className="mt-4 rounded-2xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 p-4">
  148:                 <summary className="cursor-pointer font-bold text-amber-900">
  149:                   Show hint
  150:                 </summary>
  151: 
  152:                 <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-amber-800">{question.hint}</p>
  153:               </details>
  154:             )}
  155: 
  156:             <input
  157:               type="text"
  158:               value={response.response}
  159:               onChange={(event) =>
  160:                 updateResponse(index, {
  161:                   response: event.target.value,
  162:                   checked: false,
  163:                   correct: false,
  164:                 })
  165:               }
  166:               placeholder="Type your answer..."
  167:               className="mt-5 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 outline-none transition focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
  168:             />
  169: 
  170:             <button
  171:               type="button"
  172:               onClick={() => checkAnswer(index)}
  173:               disabled={!response.response.trim()}
  174:               className="mt-4 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-5 py-3 font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-slate-300"
  175:             >
  176:               Check answer
  177:             </button>
  178: 
  179:             {response.checked && (
  180:               <div
  181:                 className={`mt-5 rounded-2xl border p-4 ${
  182:                   response.correct
  183:                     ? "border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-900"
  184:                     : "border-red-200 bg-red-50 text-red-900"
  185:                 }`}
  186:               >
  187:                 <p className="font-black">
  188:                   {response.correct ? "âœ… Correct" : "âŒ Not quite"}
  189:                 </p>
  190: 
  191:                 <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6">
  192:                   {response.correct
  193:                     ? question.feedback || "Excellent work."
  194:                     : "Review the explanation and try the question again."}
  195:                 </p>
  196: 
  197:                 {!response.correct && (
  198:                   <details className="mt-3">
  199:                     <summary className="cursor-pointer text-sm font-bold">
  200:                       Show correct answer
  201:                     </summary>
  202: 
  203:                     <p className="mt-2 text-sm">{question.answer}</p>
  204:                   </details>
  205:                 )}
  206:               </div>
  207:             )}
  208:           </article>
  209:         );
  210:       })}
  211:     </div>
  212:   );
  213: }

   35: const DEFAULT_QUIZ_DURATION_SECONDS = 8 * 60;
   36: const FULLSCREEN_EXIT_COUNTDOWN_SECONDS = 5;
   37: 
   38: function parseQuizDurationSeconds(value: string): number {
   39:   const normalised = value.trim().toLowerCase();
   40: 
   41:   if (!normalised) {
   42:     return DEFAULT_QUIZ_DURATION_SECONDS;
   43:   }
   44: 
   45:   const hoursMatch = normalised.match(
   46:     /(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?:hours?|hrs?|hr|h)\b/,
   47:   );
   48: 
   49:   const minutesMatch = normalised.match(
   50:     /(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?:minutes?|mins?|min|m)\b/,
   51:   );
   52: 
   53:   const secondsMatch = normalised.match(
   54:     /(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?:seconds?|secs?|sec|s)\b/,
   55:   );
   56: 
   57:   const hours = hoursMatch ? Number(hoursMatch[1]) : 0;
   58:   const minutes = minutesMatch ? Number(minutesMatch[1]) : 0;
   59:   const seconds = secondsMatch ? Number(secondsMatch[1]) : 0;
   60: 
   61:   const totalSeconds = Math.round(
   62:     hours * 60 * 60 +
   63:       minutes * 60 +
   64:       seconds,
   65:   );
   66: 
   67:   return totalSeconds > 0
   68:     ? totalSeconds
   69:     : DEFAULT_QUIZ_DURATION_SECONDS;
   70: }
   71: 
   72: function getGrade(scorePercent: number) {
   73:   if (scorePercent >= 90) return "Grade 9";
   74:   if (scorePercent >= 80) return "Grade 8";
   75:   if (scorePercent >= 70) return "Grade 7";
   76:   if (scorePercent >= 60) return "Grade 6";
   77:   if (scorePercent >= 50) return "Grade 5";
   78:   if (scorePercent >= 40) return "Grade 4";
   79:   if (scorePercent >= 30) return "Grade 3";
   80:   if (scorePercent >= 20) return "Grade 2";
   81:   return "Grade 1";
   82: }
   83: 
   84: function getMessage(scorePercent: number) {
   85:   if (scorePercent >= 90) return "Outstanding performance!";
   86:   if (scorePercent >= 75) return "Excellent work!";
   87:   if (scorePercent >= 60) return "Good progress!";
   88:   if (scorePercent >= 40) return "You are getting there.";
   89:   return "Keep practising. You can improve this.";
   90: }
   91: 
   92: function createIncident(
   93:   type: QuizIntegrityIncident["type"],
   94:   questionNumber: number | null,
   95:   detail: string,
   96: ): QuizIntegrityIncident {
   97:   return {
   98:     id: `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9)}`,
   99:     type,
  100:     occurredAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  101:     questionNumber,
  102:     detail,
  103:   };
  104: }

  106: export default function QuizPlayer({ quiz }: Props) {

  109:

  111: 
  112:   const quizDurationSeconds = useMemo(
  113:     () => parseQuizDurationSeconds(quiz.estimatedTime),
  114:     [quiz.estimatedTime],
  115:   );
  116: 
  117:   const assessmentRootRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  118:   const quizSaveInProgress = useRef(false);
  119:   const assignmentSaveInProgress = useRef(false);
  120:   const finishingRef = useRef(false);
  121: 
  122:   /*
  123:    * Synchronous integrity termination flag.
  124:    *
  125:    * React state updates are asynchronous. This ref makes the zero-XP rule
  126:    * available immediately to save operations when auto-submit occurs.
  127:    */
  128:   const integrityTerminatedRef = useRef(false);
  129: 
  130:   const fullscreenExitActiveRef = useRef(false);
  131:   const countdownTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null>(null);
  132:   const integrityIncidentsRef = useRef<QuizIntegrityIncident[]>([]);
  133: 
  134:   const [currentIndex, setCurrentIndex] = useState(0);
  135:   const [answers, setAnswers] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  136:   const [showResults, setShowResults] = useState(false);
  137:   const [timeLeft, setTimeLeft] = useState(() => quizDurationSeconds);
  138:   const [resultSaved, setResultSaved] = useState(false);
  139:   const [assignmentResultSaved, setAssignmentResultSaved] = useState(false);
  140: 
  141:   const [linkedAssignment, setLinkedAssignment] =
  142:     useState<LinkedAssignment | null>(null);
  143:   const [assignmentLoading, setAssignmentLoading] = useState(Boolean(assignmentId));
  144:   const [assignmentError, setAssignmentError] = useState("");
  145: 
  146:   const [integrityStarted, setIntegrityStarted] = useState(false);
  147:   const [integritySessionStartedAt, setIntegritySessionStartedAt] =
  148:     useState<string | null>(null);
  149:   const [fullscreenCountdown, setFullscreenCountdown] =
  150:     useState<number | null>(null);
  151:   const [integrityWarning, setIntegrityWarning] = useState("");
  152:   const [integrityTerminated, setIntegrityTerminated] = useState(false);
  153:   const [integrityTerminationReason, setIntegrityTerminationReason] =
  154:     useState("");
  155: 
  156:   const deliveryMode: QuizDeliveryMode =
  157:     linkedAssignment?.deliveryMode ?? "practice";
  158:   const assessmentMode =
  159:     Boolean(assignmentId) && deliveryMode === "assessment";
  160: 
  161:   const currentQuestion = quiz.questions[currentIndex];
  162:   const selectedAnswer = currentQuestion
  163:     ? answers[currentQuestion.id] || ""
  164:     : "";
  165: 
  166:   const currentQuestionNumber = currentIndex + 1;
  167: 
  168:   const progress = Math.round(
  169:     ((currentIndex + 1) / Math.max(1, quiz.questions.length)) * 100,
  170:   );
  171: 
  172:   const totalXP = useMemo(() => {
  173:     return quiz.questions.reduce(
  174:       (total, question) => total + question.xpReward,
  175:       0,
  176:     );
  177:   }, [quiz.questions]);
  178: 
  179:   const correctCount = quiz.questions.filter((question) => {
  180:     const userAnswer = answers[question.id]?.trim().toLowerCase();
  181:     const correctAnswer = question.correctAnswer.trim().toLowerCase();
  182:     return userAnswer === correctAnswer;
  183:   }).length;
  184: 
  185:   const scorePercent = Math.round(
  186:     (correctCount / Math.max(1, quiz.questions.length)) * 100,
  187:   );
  188: 
  189:   const earnedXP = quiz.questions.reduce((total, question) => {
  190:     const userAnswer = answers[question.id]?.trim().toLowerCase();
  191:     const correctAnswer = question.correctAnswer.trim().toLowerCase();
  192: 
  193:     return userAnswer === correctAnswer
  194:       ? total + question.xpReward
  195:       : total;
  196:   }, 0);
  197: 
  198:   /*
  199:    * Integrity-terminated assessment attempts retain their raw score for
  200:    * teacher evidence, but do not award XP.
  201:    */
  202:   const awardedXP = integrityTerminated ? 0 : earnedXP;
  203: 
  204:   const strengths = quiz.questions
  205:     .filter((question) => {
  206:       const userAnswer = answers[question.id]?.trim().toLowerCase();
  207:       const correctAnswer = question.correctAnswer.trim().toLowerCase();
  208:       return userAnswer === correctAnswer;
  209:     })
  210:     .slice(0, 3);
  211: 
  212:   const needsPractice = quiz.questions
  213:     .filter((question) => {
  214:       const userAnswer = answers[question.id]?.trim().toLowerCase();
  215:       const correctAnswer = question.correctAnswer.trim().toLowerCase();
  216:       return userAnswer !== correctAnswer;
  217:     })
  218:     .slice(0, 3);
  219: 
  220:   useEffect(() => {
  221:     let cancelled = false;
  222: 
  223:     async function loadLinkedAssignment() {
  224:       if (!assignmentId) {
  225:         setLinkedAssignment(null);
  226:         setAssignmentLoading(false);
  227:         return;
  228:       }
  229: 
  230:       try {
  231:         setAssignmentLoading(true);
  232:         setAssignmentError("");
  233: 
  234:         const snapshot = await getDoc(doc(db, "assignments", assignmentId));
  235: 
  236:         if (!snapshot.exists()) {
  237:           throw new Error("The linked quiz assignment could not be found.");
  238:         }
  239: 
  240:         const data = snapshot.data();
  241: 
  242:         if (data.type !== "quiz") {
  243:           throw new Error("This assignment is not a quiz assignment.");
  244:         }
  245: 
  246:         if (!data.classId || !data.teacherId) {
  247:           throw new Error(
  248:             "The assignment is missing its class or teacher information.",
  249:           );
  250:         }
  251: 
  252:         if (!cancelled) {
  253:           setLinkedAssignment({
  254:             id: snapshot.id,
  255:             classId: data.classId,
  256:             teacherId: data.teacherId,
  257:             resourceId: data.resourceId || quiz.topicId,
  258:             deliveryMode:
  259:               data.deliveryMode === "assessment" ? "assessment" : "practice",
  260:           });
  261:         }
  262:       } catch (error) {
  263:         console.error("Quiz assignment load error:", error);
  264: 
  265:         if (!cancelled) {
  266:           setAssignmentError(
  267:             error instanceof Error
  268:               ? error.message
  269:               : "The linked quiz assignment could not be loaded.",
  270:           );
  271:         }
  272:       } finally {
  273:         if (!cancelled) {
  274:           setAssignmentLoading(false);
  275:         }
  276:       }
  277:     }
  278: 
  279:     void loadLinkedAssignment();
  280: 
  281:     return () => {
  282:       cancelled = true;
  283:     };
  284:   }, [assignmentId, quiz.topicId]);
  285: 
  286:   useEffect(() => {
  287:     setTimeLeft(quizDurationSeconds);
  288:   }, [quiz.id, quizDurationSeconds]);
  289: 
  290:   useEffect(() => {
  291:     return () => {
  292:       if (countdownTimerRef.current) {
  293:         clearInterval(countdownTimerRef.current);
  294:       }
  295:     };
  296:   }, []);
  297: 
  298:   function appendIntegrityIncident(
  299:     type: QuizIntegrityIncident["type"],
  300:     detail: string,
  301:   ) {
  302:     const incident = createIncident(
  303:       type,

  305:       detail,
  306:     );
  307:

  309:       ...integrityIncidentsRef.current,

  311:     ];
  312:   }

  314:   async function leaveFullscreenSafely() {
  315:     if (document.fullscreenElement) {
  316:       await document.exitFullscreen().catch(() => undefined);
  317:     }
  318:   }
  319: 
  320:   async function finishQuiz({
  321:     terminated = false,
  322:     reason = "",
  323:   }: {
  324:     terminated?: boolean;
  325:     reason?: string;
  326:   } = {}) {
  327:     if (finishingRef.current || showResults) {
  328:       return;
  329:     }
  330: 
  331:     finishingRef.current = true;
  332: 
  333:     if (countdownTimerRef.current) {
  334:       clearInterval(countdownTimerRef.current);
  335:       countdownTimerRef.current = null;
  336:     }
  337: 
  338:     fullscreenExitActiveRef.current = false;
  339:     setFullscreenCountdown(null);
  340: 
  341:     if (terminated) {
  342:       /*
  343:        * Set the ref first so save effects see termination synchronously,
  344:        * before React finishes applying the state update.
  345:        */
  346:       integrityTerminatedRef.current = true;
  347:       setIntegrityTerminated(true);
  348:       setIntegrityTerminationReason(reason);
  349: 
  350:       appendIntegrityIncident(
  351:         "auto_submit",
  352:         reason || "The assessment was automatically submitted.",
  353:       );
  354:     }
  355: 
  356:     await leaveFullscreenSafely();
  357:     setShowResults(true);
  358:   }
  359: 
  360:   useEffect(() => {
  361:     if (
  362:       showResults ||
  363:       assignmentLoading ||
  364:       (assessmentMode && !integrityStarted)
  365:     ) {
  366:       return;
  367:     }
  368: 
  369:     const timer = window.setInterval(() => {
  370:       setTimeLeft((previousTime) => {
  371:         if (previousTime <= 1) {
  372:           window.clearInterval(timer);
  373: 
  374:           void finishQuiz({
  375:             terminated: assessmentMode,
  376:             reason: assessmentMode
  377:               ? "The assessment timer expired and the quiz was automatically submitted."
  378:               : "",
  379:           });
  380: 
  381:           return 0;
  382:         }
  383: 
  384:         return previousTime - 1;
  385:       });
  386:     }, 1000);
  387: 
  388:     return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  389:   }, [
  390:     showResults,
  391:     assignmentLoading,
  392:     assessmentMode,
  393:     integrityStarted,
  394:   ]);
  395: 
  396:   function beginFullscreenCountdown() {
  397:     if (
  398:       !assessmentMode ||
  399:       !integrityStarted ||
  400:       showResults ||
  401:       finishingRef.current ||
  402:       fullscreenExitActiveRef.current
  403:     ) {
  404:       return;
  405:     }
  406: 
  407:     fullscreenExitActiveRef.current = true;
  408: 
  409:     setFullscreenCountdown(FULLSCREEN_EXIT_COUNTDOWN_SECONDS);
  410: 
  411:     appendIntegrityIncident(
  412:       "fullscreen_exit",
  413:       `Fullscreen was exited. A ${FULLSCREEN_EXIT_COUNTDOWN_SECONDS}-second return countdown started.`,
  414:     );
  415: 
  416:     let remaining = FULLSCREEN_EXIT_COUNTDOWN_SECONDS;
  417: 
  418:     countdownTimerRef.current = setInterval(() => {
  419:       remaining -= 1;
  420:       setFullscreenCountdown(Math.max(remaining, 0));
  421: 
  422:       if (remaining <= 0) {
  423:         if (countdownTimerRef.current) {
  424:           clearInterval(countdownTimerRef.current);
  425:           countdownTimerRef.current = null;
  426:         }
  427: 
  428:         void finishQuiz({
  429:           terminated: true,
  430:           reason:
  431:             "The learner exited fullscreen and did not return within 5 seconds.",
  432:         });
  433:       }
  434:     }, 1000);
  435:   }
  436: 
  437:   function resolveFullscreenExit() {
  438:     if (!fullscreenExitActiveRef.current) {
  439:       return;
  440:     }
  441: 
  442:     fullscreenExitActiveRef.current = false;
  443: 
  444:     if (countdownTimerRef.current) {
  445:       clearInterval(countdownTimerRef.current);
  446:       countdownTimerRef.current = null;
  447:     }
  448: 
  449:     setFullscreenCountdown(null);
  450: 
  451:     appendIntegrityIncident(
  452:       "fullscreen_restored",
  453:       "Fullscreen was restored before the five-second termination countdown expired.",
  454:     );
  455:   }
  456: 
  457:   useEffect(() => {
  458:     if (
  459:       !assessmentMode ||
  460:       !integrityStarted ||
  461:       showResults
  462:     ) {
  463:       return;
  464:     }
  465: 
  466:     function onFullscreenChange() {
  467:       if (finishingRef.current) {
  468:         return;
  469:       }
  470: 
  471:       if (
  472:         document.fullscreenElement ===
  473:         assessmentRootRef.current
  474:       ) {
  475:         resolveFullscreenExit();
  476:       } else {
  477:         beginFullscreenCountdown();
  478:       }
  479:     }
  480: 
  481:     function onVisibilityChange() {
  482:       if (finishingRef.current) {
  483:         return;
  484:       }
  485: 
  486:       if (document.visibilityState === "hidden") {
  487:         appendIntegrityIncident(
  488:           "page_hidden",
  489:           "The assessment page became hidden.",
  490:         );
  491: 
  492:         setIntegrityWarning(
  493:           "The assessment page was hidden. This incident has been recorded.",
  494:         );
  495:       } else {
  496:         appendIntegrityIncident(
  497:           "page_visible",
  498:           "The assessment page became visible again.",
  499:         );
  500:       }
  501:     }
  502: 
  503:     document.addEventListener(
  504:       "fullscreenchange",
  505:       onFullscreenChange,
  506:     );
  507:     document.addEventListener(
  508:       "visibilitychange",
  509:       onVisibilityChange,
  510:     );
  511: 
  512:     return () => {
  513:       document.removeEventListener(
  514:         "fullscreenchange",
  515:         onFullscreenChange,
  516:       );
  517:       document.removeEventListener(
  518:         "visibilitychange",
  519:         onVisibilityChange,
  520:       );
  521:     };
  522:   }, [
  523:     assessmentMode,
  524:     integrityStarted,
  525:     showResults,
  526:     currentIndex,
  527:   ]);
  528: 
  529:   async function enterAssessmentMode() {
  530:     if (!assessmentRootRef.current) {
  531:       return;
  532:     }
  533: 
  534:     if (!document.fullscreenEnabled) {
  535:       toast.error(
  536:         "Fullscreen is not available in this browser. Ask your teacher for support.",
  537:       );
  538:       return;
  539:     }
  540: 
  541:     try {
  542:       await assessmentRootRef.current.requestFullscreen();
  543: 
  544:       const startedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  545:       setIntegritySessionStartedAt(startedAt);
  546:       setIntegrityStarted(true);
  547:       setIntegrityWarning("");
  548:       integrityTerminatedRef.current = false;
  549:       setIntegrityTerminated(false);
  550:       setIntegrityTerminationReason("");
  551:       finishingRef.current = false;
  552:       integrityIncidentsRef.current = [];
  553: 
  554:       toast.success("Monitored assessment started.");
  555:     } catch (error) {
  556:       console.error(
  557:         "Unable to enter monitored assessment mode:",
  558:         error,
  559:       );
  560: 
  561:       toast.error(
  562:         "Fullscreen assessment mode could not be started.",
  563:       );
  564:     }
  565:   }
  566: 
  567:   async function returnToFullscreen() {
  568:     if (!assessmentRootRef.current) {
  569:       return;
  570:     }
  571: 
  572:     try {
  573:       await assessmentRootRef.current.requestFullscreen();
  574:     } catch {
  575:       toast.error(
  576:         "Fullscreen could not be restored. Return before the countdown reaches zero.",
  577:       );
  578:     }
  579:   }
  580: 
  581:   useEffect(() => {
  582:     async function saveNormalQuizResult() {
  583:       if (
  584:         !showResults ||
  585:         resultSaved ||
  586:         !user ||
  587:         quizSaveInProgress.current
  588:       ) {
  589:         return;
  590:       }
  591: 
  592:       quizSaveInProgress.current = true;
  593: 
  594:       try {
  595:         await saveQuizResult({
  596:           uid: user.uid,
  597:           quizId: quiz.id,
  598:           topicId: quiz.topicId,
  599:           title: quiz.title,
  600:           scorePercent,
  601:           correctCount,
  602:           totalQuestions: quiz.questions.length,
  603:           earnedXP:
  604:             integrityTerminatedRef.current
  605:               ? 0
  606:               : awardedXP,
  607:         });
  608: 
  609:         setResultSaved(true);
  610: 
  611:         toast.success(
  612:           integrityTerminated
  613:             ? "Assessment result saved. No XP was awarded."
  614:             : `Quiz result saved! +${awardedXP} XP`,
  615:         );
  616:       } catch (error) {
  617:         console.error("Quiz save error:", error);
  618:         toast.error("Could not save quiz result.");
  619:       } finally {
  620:         quizSaveInProgress.current = false;
  621:       }
  622:     }
  623: 
  624:     void saveNormalQuizResult();
  625:   }, [
  626:     showResults,
  627:     resultSaved,
  628:     user,
  629:     quiz.id,
  630:     quiz.topicId,
  631:     quiz.title,
  632:     quiz.questions.length,
  633:     scorePercent,
  634:     correctCount,
  635:     awardedXP,
  636:     integrityTerminated,
  637:   ]);
  638: 
  639:   useEffect(() => {
  640:     async function saveLinkedAssignmentResult() {
  641:       if (
  642:         !showResults ||
  643:         !assignmentId ||
  644:         !linkedAssignment ||
  645:         assignmentResultSaved ||
  646:         !user ||
  647:         assignmentSaveInProgress.current
  648:       ) {
  649:         return;
  650:       }
  651: 
  652:       assignmentSaveInProgress.current = true;
  653: 
  654:       try {
  655:         await saveAssignmentResult({
  656:           assignmentId,
  657:           studentId: user.uid,
  658:           classId: linkedAssignment.classId,
  659:           teacherId: linkedAssignment.teacherId,
  660:           assignmentType: "quiz",
  661:           resourceId: linkedAssignment.resourceId || quiz.topicId,
  662:           score: correctCount,
  663:           totalQuestions: quiz.questions.length,
  664:           percentage: scorePercent,
  665:           earnedXP:
  666:             integrityTerminatedRef.current
  667:               ? 0
  668:               : awardedXP,
  669:           timeTakenSeconds: Math.max(0, quizDurationSeconds - timeLeft),
  670: 
  671:           ...(assessmentMode
  672:             ? {
  673:                 deliveryMode: "assessment" as const,
  674:                 integritySessionStartedAt,
  675:                 integrityIncidents: integrityIncidentsRef.current,
  676:                 integrityTerminated,
  677:                 integrityTerminationReason,
  678:               }
  679:             : {
  680:                 deliveryMode: "practice" as const,
  681:               }),
  682:         });
  683: 
  684:         setAssignmentResultSaved(true);
  685: 
  686:         toast.success(
  687:           integrityTerminated
  688:             ? "Assessment automatically submitted."
  689:             : "Assignment marked as completed.",
  690:         );
  691:       } catch (error) {
  692:         console.error("Assignment result save error:", error);
  693: 
  694:         toast.error(
  695:           error instanceof Error
  696:             ? error.message
  697:             : "Could not mark the assignment as completed.",
  698:         );
  699:       } finally {
  700:         assignmentSaveInProgress.current = false;
  701:       }
  702:     }
  703: 
  704:     void saveLinkedAssignmentResult();
  705:   }, [
  706:     showResults,
  707:     assignmentId,
  708:     linkedAssignment,
  709:     assignmentResultSaved,
  710:     user,
  711:     quiz.topicId,
  712:     quiz.questions.length,
  713:     correctCount,
  714:     scorePercent,
  715:     awardedXP,
  716:     timeLeft,
  717:     quizDurationSeconds,
  718:     assessmentMode,
  719:     integritySessionStartedAt,
  720:     integrityTerminated,
  721:     integrityTerminationReason,
  722:   ]);
  723: 
  724:   function formatTime(seconds: number) {
  725:     const minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
  726:     const remainingSeconds = seconds % 60;
  727: 
  728:     return `${minutes}:${remainingSeconds
  729:       .toString()
  730:       .padStart(2, "0")}`;
  731:   }
  732: 
  733:   function saveAnswer(answer: string) {
  734:     if (assessmentMode && !integrityStarted) {
  735:       return;
  736:     }
  737: 
  738:     setAnswers((previousAnswers) => ({
  739:       ...previousAnswers,
  740:       [currentQuestion.id]: answer,
  741:     }));
  742:   }
  743: 
  744:   function goNext() {
  745:     if (currentIndex < quiz.questions.length - 1) {
  746:       setCurrentIndex((previousIndex) => previousIndex + 1);
  747:       setIntegrityWarning("");
  748:       return;
  749:     }
  750: 
  751:     void finishQuiz();
  752:   }
  753: 
  754:   function goPrevious() {
  755:     if (currentIndex > 0) {
  756:       setCurrentIndex((previousIndex) => previousIndex - 1);
  757:       setIntegrityWarning("");
  758:     }
  759:   }
  760: 
  761:   if (assignmentLoading) {
  762:     return (
  763:       <Card>
  764:         <p className="font-bold text-slate-700">
  765:           Preparing your assigned quiz...
  766:         </p>

  787:     !showResults &&
  788:     !integrityStarted
