 1185:         <div className="rounded-2xl border border-indigo-200 bg-indigo-50 px-5 py-4">
 1186:           <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-indigo-900">
 1187:             <ShieldCheck className="h-5 w-5" />
 1188:             <p className="font-black">
 1189:               Monitored assessment in progress
 1190:             </p>
 1191:           </div>
 1192: 
 1193:           <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-indigo-800">
 1194:             Fullscreen and page visibility events are being recorded.
 1195:           </p>
 1196:         </div>
 1197:       )}
 1198: 
 1199:       {integrityWarning && assessmentMode && (
 1200:         <div className="rounded-2xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 p-4 text-sm font-semibold text-amber-900">
 1201:           {integrityWarning}
 1202:         </div>
 1203:       )}
 1204: 
 1205:       <Card className="border-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-slate-900 to-blue-700 text-white">
 1206:         <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-blue-200">
 1207:           {assessmentMode ? "Monitored Quiz Assessment" : "Quiz"}
 1208:         </p>
 1209: 
 1210:         <h1 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold">
 1211:           {quiz.title}
 1212:         </h1>
 1213: 
 1214:         <p className="mt-2 text-blue-100">
 1215:           {quiz.description}
 1216:         </p>
 1217: 
 1218:         <div className="mt-6">
 1219:           <div className="mb-2 flex justify-between text-sm font-semibold text-blue-100">
 1220:             <span>
 1221:               Question {currentIndex + 1} of {quiz.questions.length}
 1222:             </span>
 1223: 
 1224:             <span>{progress}% complete</span>
 1225:           </div>
 1226: 
 1227:           <ProgressBar value={progress} />
 1228:         </div>
 1229: 
 1230:         <div className="mt-6 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
 1231:           <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white/10 p-4">
 1232:             <p className="text-sm text-blue-100">
 1233:               â± Time Left
 1234:             </p>
 1235: 
 1236:             <p className="mt-1 text-2xl font-bold">
 1237:               {formatTime(timeLeft)}
 1238:             </p>
 1239:           </div>
 1240: 
 1241:           <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white/10 p-4">
 1242:             <p className="text-sm text-blue-100">
 1243:               â­ XP Available
 1244:             </p>
 1245: 
 1246:             <p className="mt-1 text-2xl font-bold">
 1247:               {totalXP}
 1248:             </p>
 1249:           </div>
 1250: 
 1251:           <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white/10 p-4">
 1252:             <p className="text-sm text-blue-100">
 1253:               ðŸ“š Estimated Time
 1254:             </p>
 1255: 
 1256:             <p className="mt-1 text-2xl font-bold">
 1257:               {quiz.estimatedTime}
 1258:             </p>
 1259:           </div>
 1260:         </div>
 1261:       </Card>
 1262: 
 1263:       <Card>
 1264:         <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-blue-600">
 1265:           Question {currentIndex + 1}
 1266:         </p>
 1267: 
 1268:         <h2 className="mt-4 text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">
 1269:           {currentQuestion.question}
 1270:         </h2>
 1271: 
 1272:         <div className="mt-8 space-y-3">
 1273:           {currentQuestion.type === "multipleChoice" ||
 1274:           currentQuestion.type === "trueFalse" ? (
 1275:             currentQuestion.options?.map((option) => (
 1276:               <button
 1277:                 key={option}
 1278:                 type="button"
 1279:                 onClick={() => saveAnswer(option)}
 1280:                 className={`w-full rounded-xl border p-4 text-left font-semibold transition ${
 1281:                   selectedAnswer === option
 1282:                     ? "border-blue-600 bg-blue-50 text-blue-700"
 1283:                     : "border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50"
 1284:                 }`}
 1285:               >
 1286:                 {option}
 1287:               </button>
 1288:             ))
 1289:           ) : (
 1290:             <input
 1291:               value={selectedAnswer}
 1292:               onChange={(event) =>
 1293:                 saveAnswer(event.target.value)
 1294:               }
 1295:               placeholder="Type your answer..."
 1296:               className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 focus:border-blue-500 focus:outline-none"
 1297:             />
 1298:           )}
 1299:         </div>
 1300: 
 1301:         <div className="mt-8 flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row">
 1302:           <Button
 1303:             variant="secondary"
 1304:             onClick={goPrevious}
 1305:             disabled={currentIndex === 0}
 1306:           >
 1307:             Previous
 1308:           </Button>
 1309: 
 1310:           <Button onClick={goNext}>
 1311:             {currentIndex === quiz.questions.length - 1
 1312:               ? "Finish Quiz"
 1313:               : "Next Question"}
 1314:           </Button>
 1315:         </div>
 1316:       </Card>
 1317:     </div>
 1318:   );
 1319: 
 1320:   if (!assessmentMode) {
 1321:     return quizContent;
 1322:   }
 1323: 
 1324:   return (
 1325:     <div
 1326:       ref={assessmentRootRef}
 1327:       className="min-h-screen overflow-y-auto bg-slate-100 p-6"
 1328:     >
 1329:       <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl">
 1330:         {quizContent}
 1331:       </div>
 1332: 
 1333:       {fullscreenCountdown !== null && (
 1334:         <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-950/90 p-6 text-white">
 1335:           <div className="w-full max-w-xl rounded-3xl border border-red-400/40 bg-slate-900 p-8 text-center shadow-2xl">
 1336:             <AlertTriangle className="mx-auto h-12 w-12 text-red-400" />
 1337: 
 1338:             <p className="mt-5 text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-red-300">
 1339:               Fullscreen exited
 1340:             </p>
 1341: 
 1342:             <p className="mt-4 text-7xl font-black">
 1343:               {fullscreenCountdown}
 1344:             </p>
 1345: 
 1346:             <p className="mt-4 text-lg font-bold">
 1347:               Return to fullscreen before the countdown reaches zero.
 1348:             </p>
 1349: 
 1350:             <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-300">
 1351:               If you do not return within five seconds, the quiz will be
 1352:               automatically submitted and the incident will be recorded.
 1353:             </p>
 1354: 
 1355:             <button
 1356:               type="button"
 1357:               onClick={() => void returnToFullscreen()}
 1358:               className="mt-7 w-full rounded-2xl bg-indigo-600 px-6 py-4 font-black text-white hover:bg-indigo-500"
 1359:             >
 1360:               Return to fullscreen
 1361:             </button>
 1362:           </div>
 1363:         </div>
 1364:       )}
 1365:     </div>
 1366:   );
 1367: }

    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
    4: 
    5: import { useProgress } from "@/contexts/ProgressContext";
    6: 
    7: import SimulatorControls from "@/components/Simulators/common/SimulatorControls";
    8: import SimulatorDifficulty from "@/components/Simulators/common/SimulatorDifficulty";
    9: import SimulatorFeedback from "@/components/Simulators/common/SimulatorFeedback";
   10: import SimulatorStats from "@/components/Simulators/common/SimulatorStats";
   11: 
   12: import {
   13:   useSimulator,
   14:   type SimulatorDifficulty as DifficultyLevel,
   15: } from "@/components/Simulators/common/useSimulator";
   16: 
   17: type Question = {
   18:   values: number[];
   19: };
   20: 
   21: type ProcedureFeedbackType = "success" | "error" | "info" | null;
   22: 
   23: function createValues(length: number): number[] {
   24:   return Array.from({ length }, () => Math.floor(Math.random() * 20) + 1);
   25: }
   26: 
   27: function createQuestion(difficulty: DifficultyLevel): Question {
   28:   return {
   29:     values: createValues(
   30:       difficulty === "foundation" ? 5 : difficulty === "intermediate" ? 6 : 7,
   31:     ),
   32:   };
   33: }
   34: 
   35: function normaliseList(value: string): number[] {
   36:   return value
   37:     .split(/[\s,]+/)
   38:     .map((item) => Number(item))
   39:     .filter((item) => Number.isFinite(item));
   40: }
   41: 
   42: export default function BubbleSortSimulator() {
   43:   const { addXP } = useProgress();
   44: 
   45:   const simulator = useSimulator<Question>({
   46:     initialQuestion: createQuestion("foundation"),
   47:     generateQuestion: createQuestion,
   48: 
   49:     xpByDifficulty: {
   50:       foundation: 10,
   51:       intermediate: 15,
   52:       higher: 20,
   53:     },
   54: 
   55:     onAwardXP: addXP,
   56:   });
   57: 
   58:   const {
   59:     difficulty,
   60:     question,
   61: 
   62:     checked,
   63:     correct,
   64: 
   65:     hintVisible,
   66:     workingVisible,
   67: 
   68:     attempts,
   69:     correctAnswers,
   70:     accuracy,
   71:     xp,
   72:     streak,
   73: 
   74:     markAnswer,
   75:     resetQuestion,
   76:     newQuestion,
   77:     changeDifficulty,
   78: 
   79:     toggleHint,
   80:     toggleWorking,
   81:   } = simulator;
   82: 
   83:   /*
   84:    * ---------------------------------------------------------
   85:    * Scored challenge
   86:    * ---------------------------------------------------------
   87:    */
   88: 
   89:   const [answer, setAnswer] = useState("");
   90: 
   91:   const sortedValues = useMemo(
   92:     () => [...question.values].sort((a, b) => a - b),
   93:     [question.values],
   94:   );
   95: 
   96:   const submittedValues = normaliseList(answer);
   97: 
   98:   const canCheck = submittedValues.length === question.values.length;
   99: 
  100:   function handleCheck() {
  101:     if (!canCheck) {
  102:       return;
  103:     }
  104: 
  105:     const matches = sortedValues.every(
  106:       (value, index) => value === submittedValues[index],
  107:     );
  108: 
  109:     markAnswer(matches);
  110:   }
  111: 
  112:   function handleTryAgain() {
  113:     setAnswer("");
  114:     resetQuestion();
  115:   }
  116: 
  117:   /*
  118:    * ---------------------------------------------------------
  119:    * Procedural trainer state
  120:    * ---------------------------------------------------------
  121:    */
  122: 
  123:   const [procedureValues, setProcedureValues] = useState<number[]>(
  124:     question.values,
  125:   );
  126: 
  127:   const [compareIndex, setCompareIndex] = useState(0);
  128:   const [pass, setPass] = useState(1);
  129: 
  130:   const [swappedInPass, setSwappedInPass] = useState(false);
  131: 
  132:   const [selectedIndices, setSelectedIndices] = useState<number[]>([]);
  133: 
  134:   const [procedureComplete, setProcedureComplete] = useState(false);
  135: 
  136:   const [awaitingEndPass, setAwaitingEndPass] = useState(false);
  137: 
  138:   const [procedureCorrectSteps, setProcedureCorrectSteps] = useState(0);
  139: 
  140:   const [procedureMistakes, setProcedureMistakes] = useState(0);
  141: 
  142:   const [passesCompleted, setPassesCompleted] = useState(0);
  143: 
  144:   const [procedureFeedback, setProcedureFeedback] = useState("");
  145: 
  146:   const [procedureFeedbackType, setProcedureFeedbackType] =
  147:     useState<ProcedureFeedbackType>(null);
  148: 
  149:   const procedureAttempts = procedureCorrectSteps + procedureMistakes;
  150: 
  151:   const procedureAccuracy =
  152:     procedureAttempts === 0
  153:       ? 0
  154:       : Math.round((procedureCorrectSteps / procedureAttempts) * 100);
  155: 
  156:   const pairLeft = compareIndex;
  157:   const pairRight = compareIndex + 1;
  158: 
  159:   const currentLeft = procedureValues[pairLeft];
  160:   const currentRight = procedureValues[pairRight];
  161: 
  162: 
  163:   const lastComparisonIndex = procedureValues.length - pass - 1;
  164: 
  165:   const currentPairIsLastInPass = compareIndex >= lastComparisonIndex;
  166: 
  167:   /*
  168:    * Foundation:
  169:    * simulator automatically identifies the pair.
  170:    *
  171:    * Intermediate / Higher:
  172:    * student selects the pair.
  173:    */
  174:   const pairReady =
  175:     difficulty === "foundation" ||
  176:     (selectedIndices.length === 2 &&
  177:       selectedIndices.includes(pairLeft) &&
  178:       selectedIndices.includes(pairRight));
  179: 
  180:   function resetProcedure(values = question.values) {
  181:     setProcedureValues([...values]);
  182:     setCompareIndex(0);
  183:     setPass(1);
  184:     setSwappedInPass(false);
  185:     setSelectedIndices([]);
  186:     setProcedureComplete(false);
  187:     setAwaitingEndPass(false);
  188: 
  189:     setProcedureCorrectSteps(0);
  190:     setProcedureMistakes(0);
  191:     setPassesCompleted(0);
  192: 
  193:     setProcedureFeedback("");
  194:     setProcedureFeedbackType(null);
  195:   }
  196: 
  197:   function handleNewQuestion() {
  198:     setAnswer("");
  199: 
  200:     /*
  201:      * We clear the old procedure immediately.
  202:      * When the hook generates the next question, the fallback rendering
  203:      * below uses question.values until the student starts the trainer.
  204:      */
  205:     setProcedureValues([]);
  206:     setCompareIndex(0);
  207:     setPass(1);
  208:     setSwappedInPass(false);
  209:     setSelectedIndices([]);
  210:     setProcedureComplete(false);
  211:     setAwaitingEndPass(false);
  212: 
  213:     setProcedureCorrectSteps(0);
  214:     setProcedureMistakes(0);
  215:     setPassesCompleted(0);
  216: 
  217:     setProcedureFeedback("");
  218:     setProcedureFeedbackType(null);
  219: 
  220:     newQuestion();
  221:   }
  222: 
  223:   function handleDifficultyChange(nextDifficulty: DifficultyLevel) {
  224:     setAnswer("");
  225: 
  226:     setProcedureValues([]);
  227:     setCompareIndex(0);
  228:     setPass(1);
  229:     setSwappedInPass(false);
  230:     setSelectedIndices([]);
  231:     setProcedureComplete(false);
  232:     setAwaitingEndPass(false);
  233: 
  234:     setProcedureCorrectSteps(0);
  235:     setProcedureMistakes(0);
  236:     setPassesCompleted(0);
  237: 
  238:     setProcedureFeedback("");
  239:     setProcedureFeedbackType(null);
  240: 
  241:     changeDifficulty(nextDifficulty);
  242:   }
  243: 
  244:   /*
  245:    * If a new question has been generated, procedureValues may temporarily
  246:    * be empty. In that case use the new question list.
  247:    */
  248:   const displayedProcedureValues =
  249:     procedureValues.length === question.values.length
  250:       ? procedureValues
  251:       : question.values;
  252: 
  253:   function ensureProcedureInitialised(): number[] {
  254:     if (procedureValues.length === question.values.length) {
  255:       return procedureValues;
  256:     }
  257: 
  258:     const initial = [...question.values];
  259: 
  260:     setProcedureValues(initial);
  261: 
  262:     return initial;
  263:   }
  264: 
  265:   function selectProcedureValue(index: number) {
  266:     if (difficulty === "foundation" || procedureComplete || awaitingEndPass) {
  267:       return;
  268:     }
  269: 
  270:     setProcedureFeedback("");
  271:     setProcedureFeedbackType(null);
  272: 
  273:     setSelectedIndices((current) => {
  274:       if (current.includes(index)) {
  275:         return current.filter((item) => item !== index);
  276:       }
  277: 
  278:       if (current.length >= 2) {
  279:         return [index];
  280:       }
  281: 
  282:       return [...current, index];
  283:     });
  284:   }
  285: 
  286:   function recordMistake(message: string) {
  287:     setProcedureMistakes((current) => current + 1);
  288:     setProcedureFeedback(message);
  289:     setProcedureFeedbackType("error");
  290:   }
  291: 
  292:   function recordSuccess(message: string) {
  293:     setProcedureCorrectSteps((current) => current + 1);
  294:     setProcedureFeedback(message);
  295:     setProcedureFeedbackType("success");
  296:   }
  297: 
  298:   function validateSelectedPair(): boolean {
  299:     if (difficulty === "foundation") {
  300:       return true;
  301:     }
  302: 
  303:     if (selectedIndices.length !== 2) {
  304:       recordMistake(
  305:         "Select exactly two adjacent values before choosing Keep or Swap.",
  306:       );
  307: 
  308:       return false;
  309:     }
  310: 
  311:     const ordered = [...selectedIndices].sort((a, b) => a - b);
  312: 
  313:     if (ordered[1] - ordered[0] !== 1) {
  314:       recordMistake(
  315:         "Bubble sort compares adjacent values only. Select two neighbouring items.",
  316:       );
  317: 
  318:       return false;
  319:     }
  320: 
  321:     if (ordered[0] !== pairLeft || ordered[1] !== pairRight) {
  322:       recordMistake(
  323:         `That is not the next comparison. Bubble sort must now compare index ${pairLeft} with index ${pairRight}.`,
  324:       );
  325: 
  326:       return false;
  327:     }
  328: 
  329:     return true;
  330:   }
  331: 
  332:   function advanceAfterCorrectDecision(didSwap: boolean, nextValues: number[]) {
  333:     const nextSwappedInPass = swappedInPass || didSwap;
  334: 
  335:     setProcedureValues(nextValues);
  336:     setSwappedInPass(nextSwappedInPass);
  337:     setSelectedIndices([]);
  338: 
  339:     /*
  340:      * We have reached the end of the current pass.
  341:      */
  342:     if (currentPairIsLastInPass) {
  343:       /*
  344:        * Higher students must explicitly recognise the end of a pass.
  345:        */
  346:       if (difficulty === "higher") {
  347:         setAwaitingEndPass(true);
  348: 
  349:         setProcedureFeedback(
  350:           "Correct decision. You have reached the end of this pass. Decide what should happen next.",
  351:         );
  352: 
  353:         setProcedureFeedbackType("info");
  354: 
  355:         return;
  356:       }
  357: 
  358:       finishPass(nextSwappedInPass, nextValues);
  359: 
  360:       return;
  361:     }
  362: 
  363:     setCompareIndex((current) => current + 1);
  364:   }
  365: 
  366:   function finishPass(hadSwap: boolean, currentValues: number[]) {
  367:     setPassesCompleted((current) => current + 1);
  368:     setAwaitingEndPass(false);
  369:     setSelectedIndices([]);
  370: 
  371:     /*
  372:      * No swap during a complete pass means the list is sorted.
  373:      */
  374:     if (!hadSwap) {
  375:       setProcedureComplete(true);
  376: 
  377:       setProcedureFeedback(
  378:         "Excellent. A complete pass produced no swaps, so the list is sorted.",
  379:       );
  380: 
  381:       setProcedureFeedbackType("success");
  382: 
  383:       return;
  384:     }
  385: 
  386:     /*
  387:      * Every pass fixes at least one value at the right-hand side.
  388:      */
  389:     if (pass >= currentValues.length - 1) {
  390:       setProcedureComplete(true);
  391: 
  392:       setProcedureFeedback(
  393:         "Excellent. All required passes are complete and the list is sorted.",
  394:       );
  395: 
  396:       setProcedureFeedbackType("success");
  397: 
  398:       return;
  399:     }
  400: 
  401:     setPass((current) => current + 1);
  402:     setCompareIndex(0);
  403:     setSwappedInPass(false);
  404: 
  405:     setProcedureFeedback(
  406:       `Pass ${pass} complete. Begin pass ${pass + 1} from the left-hand side.`,
  407:     );
  408: 
  409:     setProcedureFeedbackType("success");
  410:   }
  411: 
  412:   function handleSwap() {
  413:     if (procedureComplete || awaitingEndPass) {
  414:       return;
  415:     }
  416: 
  417:     const currentValues = ensureProcedureInitialised();
  418: 
  419:     if (!validateSelectedPair()) {
  420:       return;
  421:     }
  422: 
  423:     const left = currentValues[pairLeft];
  424:     const right = currentValues[pairRight];
  425: 
  426:     if (left === undefined || right === undefined) {
  427:       return;
  428:     }
  429: 
  430:     if (left <= right) {
  431:       recordMistake(
  432:         `${left} is not greater than ${right}. These values are already in ascending order, so they should be kept.`,
  433:       );
  434: 
  435:       return;
  436:     }
  437: 
  438:     const nextValues = [...currentValues];
  439: 
  440:     [nextValues[pairLeft], nextValues[pairRight]] = [
  441:       nextValues[pairRight],
  442:       nextValues[pairLeft],
  443:     ];
  444: 
  445:     recordSuccess(
  446:       `Correct. ${left} > ${right}, so the adjacent values must be swapped.`,
  447:     );
  448: 
  449:     advanceAfterCorrectDecision(true, nextValues);
  450:   }
  451: 
  452:   function handleKeep() {
  453:     if (procedureComplete || awaitingEndPass) {
  454:       return;
  455:     }
  456: 
  457:     const currentValues = ensureProcedureInitialised();
  458: 
  459:     if (!validateSelectedPair()) {
  460:       return;
  461:     }
  462: 
  463:     const left = currentValues[pairLeft];
  464:     const right = currentValues[pairRight];
  465: 
  466:     if (left === undefined || right === undefined) {
  467:       return;
  468:     }
  469: 
  470:     if (left > right) {
  471:       recordMistake(
  472:         `${left} > ${right}. Bubble sort requires these adjacent values to be swapped.`,
  473:       );
  474: 
  475:       return;
  476:     }
  477: 
  478:     recordSuccess(
  479:       `Correct. ${left} â‰¤ ${right}, so the values remain in this order.`,
  480:     );
  481: 
  482:     advanceAfterCorrectDecision(false, [...currentValues]);
  483:   }
  484: 
  485:   function handleEndPass() {
  486:     if (difficulty !== "higher" || procedureComplete) {
  487:       return;
  488:     }
  489: 
  490:     if (!awaitingEndPass) {
  491:       recordMistake(
  492:         "The pass is not finished yet. Continue comparing the next adjacent pair.",
  493:       );
  494: 
  495:       return;
  496:     }
  497: 
  498:     setProcedureCorrectSteps((current) => current + 1);
  499: 
  500:     finishPass(
  501:       swappedInPass ||
  502:         /*
  503:          * If the final comparison was a swap, procedureValues has already
  504:          * changed. We can determine whether the current pass contained a
  505:          * swap by comparing against the sorted state through swappedInPass,
  506:          * but the final action may have been the first swap in the pass.
  507:          *
  508:          * The safest indicator is that the list at this point differs from
  509:          * the pass-start ordering only through valid swaps. For the purpose
  510:          * of early termination, a pass with any final swap must continue.
  511:          *
  512:          * currentPairIsLastInPass plus shouldSwap before mutation cannot be
  513:          * used here after state updates, so we conservatively treat an end
  514:          * pass as having swaps unless swappedInPass is false AND the list is
  515:          * currently sorted.
  516:          */
  517:         !displayedProcedureValues.every(
  518:           (value, index, array) => index === 0 || array[index - 1] <= value,
  519:         ),
  520:       displayedProcedureValues,
  521:     );
  522:   }
  523: 
  524:   const working = `Starting list:
  525: 
  526: ${question.values.join(", ")}
  527: 
  528: Bubble sort procedure:
  529: 
  530: 1. Start at the left of the list.
  531: 2. Compare two adjacent values.
  532: 3. If the left value is greater than the right value, swap them.
  533: 4. Otherwise keep them in the same order.
  534: 5. Move one position to the right.
  535: 6. Continue until the end of the pass.
  536: 7. Repeat passes until a full pass produces no swaps.
  537: 
  538: Final sorted list:
  539: 
  540: ${sortedValues.join(", ")}`;
  541: 
  542:   const automaticPairIndices =
  543:     difficulty === "foundation" ? [pairLeft, pairRight] : [];
  544: 
  545:   const highlightedIndices =
  546:     difficulty === "foundation" ? automaticPairIndices : selectedIndices;
  547: 
  548:   return (
  549:     <section className="space-y-8 rounded-3xl border border-blue-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm md:p-8">
  550:       <header>
  551:         <p className="text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-blue-600">
  552:           Algorithm laboratory
  553:         </p>
  554: 
  555:         <h2 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-black">Bubble Sort Challenge</h2>
  556: 
  557:         <p className="mt-3 max-w-3xl leading-7 text-slate-600">
  558:           Predict the final order, then perform Bubble Sort yourself using the
  559:           correct comparisons, swaps and passes.
  560:         </p>
  561:       </header>
  562: 
  563:       <SimulatorDifficulty
  564:         value={difficulty}
  565:         onChange={handleDifficultyChange}
  566:       />
  567: 
  568:       <SimulatorStats
  569:         attempts={attempts}
  570:         correct={correctAnswers}
  571:         accuracy={accuracy}
  572:         xp={xp}
  573:         streak={streak}
  574:       />
  575: 
  576:       {/* ---------------------------------------------------- */}
  577:       {/* Scored final-list challenge                           */}
  578:       {/* ---------------------------------------------------- */}
  579: 
  580:       <section className="rounded-3xl border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 p-6">
  581:         <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-blue-600">
  582:           Final-order challenge
  583:         </p>
  584: 
  585:         <h3 className="mt-2 text-xl font-black">
  586:           Sort this list into ascending order.
  587:         </h3>
  588: 
  589:         <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
  590:           {question.values.map((value, itemIndex) => (

  598:         </div>

  617:         />
  618:

  748:             {displayedProcedureValues.map((value, itemIndex) => {
  749:               const selected = highlightedIndices.includes(itemIndex);
  750: 
  751:               const fixed =
  752:                 itemIndex > displayedProcedureValues.length - pass - 1;

  787:                 You have reached the end of this pass.

  792:                 or swap it.
  793:               </p>

  797:                 compare.
  798:               </p>

  803:             ) : (
  804:               <p>
  805:                 Decide whether this is the correct pair for the next comparison.
  806:               </p>

  808:           </div>

  820:               >

  828:               >

  830:               </button>
  831:             </>
  832:           )}
  833: 
  834:           {difficulty === "higher" && awaitingEndPass && !procedureComplete && (
  835:             <button
  836:               type="button"
  837:               onClick={handleEndPass}
  838:               className="rounded-xl bg-violet-600 px-6 py-3 font-black text-white transition hover:bg-violet-700"
  839:             >
  840:               End pass â†’
  841:             </button>
  842:           )}
  843:

  845:             type="button"
  846:             onClick={() => resetProcedure()}
  847:             className="rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-6 py-3 font-black text-slate-800"
  848:           >
  849:             Reset procedure
  850:           </button>

  857:             className={`mt-6 rounded-2xl border p-5 ${
  858:               procedureFeedbackType === "success"

  860:                 : procedureFeedbackType === "error"
  861:                   ? "border-red-300 bg-red-50 text-red-900"
  862:                   : "border-blue-300 bg-blue-50 text-blue-900"
  863:             }`}
  864:           >
  865:             <p className="font-black">
  866:               {procedureFeedbackType === "success"
  867:                 ? "âœ“ Correct procedure"
  868:                 : procedureFeedbackType === "error"
  869:                   ? "âœ• Try that step again"
  870:                   : "Procedure update"}
  871:             </p>
  872: 
  873:             <p className="mt-2 leading-7">{procedureFeedback}</p>

  884:               Bubble Sort completed successfully
  885:             </h4>
  886: 
  887:             <div className="mt-5 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
  888:               <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-4">

  890:                   Final list
  891:                 </p>

  894:                   {displayedProcedureValues.join(", ")}
  895:                 </p>
  896:               </div>
  897:

  899:                 <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase text-slate-500">

  912:               </div>
  913:             </div>
  914:           </section>
  915:         )}
  916:       </div>
  917:     </section>
  918:   );
  919: }

====================================================================================================
FILE: hooks\useProgrammingProgress.ts
====================================================================================================

    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
    4: 
    5: import { useAuth } from "@/contexts/AuthContext";
    6: import { useProgress } from "@/contexts/ProgressContext";
    7: import type {
    8:   ProgrammingChallenge,
    9:   ProgrammingProgressSnapshot,
   10:   ProgrammingSkill,
   11:   ProgrammingSkillProgress,
   12: } from "@/types/programming";
   13: 
   14: const LEGACY_STORAGE_KEY = "cs-master-programming-progress-v2";
   15: 
   16: function storageKey(studentId: string): string {
   17:   return `cs-master-programming-progress-v3:${studentId}`;
   18: }
   19: 
   20: const emptyProgress: ProgrammingProgressSnapshot = {
   21:   attempts: 0,
   22:   correct: 0,
   23:   xp: 0,
   24:   streak: 0,
   25:   bestStreak: 0,
   26:   completedChallengeIds: [],
   27:   history: [],
   28:   skillProgress: {},
   29: };
   30: 
   31: function loadProgress(studentId: string): ProgrammingProgressSnapshot {
   32:   if (typeof window === "undefined" || !studentId) return emptyProgress;
   33: 
   34:   try {
   35:     const scopedKey = storageKey(studentId);
   36:     const scoped = window.localStorage.getItem(scopedKey);
   37:     const legacy = window.localStorage.getItem(LEGACY_STORAGE_KEY);
   38:     const raw = scoped ?? legacy;
   39: 
   40:     if (!raw) return emptyProgress;
   41: 
   42:     if (!scoped && legacy) {
   43:       window.localStorage.setItem(scopedKey, legacy);
   44:       window.localStorage.removeItem(LEGACY_STORAGE_KEY);
   45:     }
   46: 
   47:     const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as Partial<ProgrammingProgressSnapshot>;
   48: 
   49:     return {
   50:       ...emptyProgress,
   51:       ...parsed,
   52:       completedChallengeIds: Array.isArray(parsed.completedChallengeIds)
   53:         ? parsed.completedChallengeIds
   54:         : [],
   55:       history: Array.isArray(parsed.history) ? parsed.history : [],
   56:       skillProgress:
   57:         parsed.skillProgress && typeof parsed.skillProgress === "object"
   58:           ? parsed.skillProgress
   59:           : {},
   60:     };
   61:   } catch {
   62:     return emptyProgress;
   63:   }
   64: }
   65: 
   66: function updateSkillProgress(
   67:   current: Partial<Record<ProgrammingSkill, ProgrammingSkillProgress>>,
   68:   skills: ProgrammingSkill[],
   69:   passed: boolean,
   70: ): Partial<Record<ProgrammingSkill, ProgrammingSkillProgress>> {
   71:   const next = { ...current };
   72: 
   73:   for (const skill of skills) {
   74:     const existing = next[skill] ?? {
   75:       attempts: 0,
   76:       correct: 0,
   77:       accuracy: 0,
   78:     };
   79: 
   80:     const attempts = existing.attempts + 1;
   81:     const correct = existing.correct + (passed ? 1 : 0);
   82: 
   83:     next[skill] = {
   84:       attempts,
   85:       correct,
   86:       accuracy: Math.round((correct / attempts) * 100),
   87:     };
   88:   }
   89: 
   90:   return next;
   91: }
   92: 
   93: export function useProgrammingProgress() {
   94:   const { user } = useAuth();
   95:   const { addXP } = useProgress();
   96:   const studentId = user?.uid ?? "";
   97:   const [progress, setProgress] =
   98:     useState<ProgrammingProgressSnapshot>(emptyProgress);
   99:   const [hydrated, setHydrated] = useState(false);
  100: 
  101:   useEffect(() => {
  102:     setHydrated(false);
  103:     setProgress(loadProgress(studentId));
  104:     setHydrated(Boolean(studentId));
  105:   }, [studentId]);
  106: 
  107:   useEffect(() => {
  108:     if (!hydrated || !studentId) return;
  109: 
  110:     window.localStorage.setItem(
  111:       storageKey(studentId),
  112:       JSON.stringify(progress),
  113:     );
  114:   }, [hydrated, progress, studentId]);
  115: 
  116:   const accuracy = useMemo(() => {
  117:     if (progress.attempts === 0) return 0;
  118: 
  119:     return Math.round(
  120:       (progress.correct / progress.attempts) * 100,
  121:     );
  122:   }, [progress.attempts, progress.correct]);
  123: 
  124:   const weakSkills = useMemo(() => {
  125:     return Object.entries(progress.skillProgress)
  126:       .filter(([, value]) => value && value.attempts >= 2)
  127:       .sort(
  128:         (a, b) =>
  129:           (a[1]?.accuracy ?? 100) - (b[1]?.accuracy ?? 100),
  130:       )
  131:       .slice(0, 4)
  132:       .map(([skill]) => skill as ProgrammingSkill);
  133:   }, [progress.skillProgress]);
  134: 
  135:   const recordAttempt = useCallback(
  136:     (challenge: ProgrammingChallenge, passed: boolean) => {
  137:       let awarded = 0;
  138: 
  139:       setProgress((current) => {
  140:         const alreadyCompleted =
  141:           current.completedChallengeIds.includes(challenge.id);
  142: 
  143:         awarded =
  144:           passed && !alreadyCompleted ? challenge.xpReward : 0;
  145: 
  146:         const nextStreak = passed ? current.streak + 1 : 0;
  147: 
  148:         return {
  149:           attempts: current.attempts + 1,
  150:           correct: current.correct + (passed ? 1 : 0),
  151:           xp: current.xp + awarded,
  152:           streak: nextStreak,
  153:           bestStreak: Math.max(current.bestStreak, nextStreak),
  154:           completedChallengeIds:
  155:             passed && !alreadyCompleted
  156:               ? [...current.completedChallengeIds, challenge.id]
  157:               : current.completedChallengeIds,
  158:           skillProgress: updateSkillProgress(
  159:             current.skillProgress,
  160:             challenge.skills,
  161:             passed,
  162:           ),
  163:           history: [
  164:             {
  165:               id: `${challenge.id}-${Date.now()}`,
  166:               challengeId: challenge.id,
  167:               challengeTitle: challenge.title,
  168:               mode: challenge.mode,
  169:               difficulty: challenge.difficulty,
  170:               skills: challenge.skills,
  171:               passed,
  172:               xpAwarded: awarded,
  173:               createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  174:             },
  175:             ...current.history,
  176:           ].slice(0, 50),
  177:         };
  178:       });
  179: 
  180:       if (awarded > 0) {
  181:         addXP(awarded);
  182:       }
  183: 
  184:       return awarded;
  185:     },
  186:     [addXP],
  187:   );
  188: 
  189:   const resetProgrammingProgress = useCallback(() => {
  190:     setProgress(emptyProgress);
  191:   }, []);
  192: 
  193:   return {
  194:     ...progress,
  195:     hydrated,
  196:     accuracy,
  197:     weakSkills,
  198:     recordAttempt,
  199:     resetProgrammingProgress,
  200:   };
  201: }
